To bridge this gap, we extend the sequence-to-sequence framework by investigating parsers built on pre-trained encoder-decoder architectures, including BART, mBART, and T5. We fine-tune them to generate linearized parse trees and extensively evaluate them on different linearization strategies across both continuous treebanks and more complex discontinuous benchmarks. Our results demonstrate that our approach outperforms all prior sequence-to-sequence models and performs competitively with leading task-specific constituent parsers on continuous constituent parsing.

</details>


### [38] [From Rosetta to Match-Up: A Paired Corpus of Linguistic Puzzles with Human and LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13408)
*Neh Majmudar,Anne Huang,Jinfan Frank Hu,Elena Filatova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将Rosetta Stone谜题系统转换为Match-Up谜题的方法，并通过人类参与者和LLM评估了两类谜题的难度模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言学谜题创作复杂且耗时，现有谜题格式转换缺乏系统性方法，因此需要一种高效的方式加速新谜题的生成。

Method: 提出一种系统化流程，将现有的Rosetta Stone谜题转换为对应的Match-Up谜题，并构建配对的谜题数据集。

Result: 人类专家求解者和LLM在Match-Up谜题上均表现出"全有或全无"的模式——要么完全解决，要么完全失败。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一个新的配对谜题数据集，并提供了跨格式谜题难度的详细评估，为理解人类和机器的语言推理能力提供了见解。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine linguistic puzzles used in high school linguistics competitions, focusing on two common formats: Rosetta Stone and Match-Up. We propose a systematic procedure for converting existing Rosetta Stone puzzles into corresponding Match-Up counterparts. Because linguistic puzzle creation is complex and time-consuming, our method provides an efficient way to accelerate the generation of new puzzles. We evaluate the resulting Rosetta Stone-Match-Up pairs with both human participants and large language models (LLMs). Our results show that both expert human solvers and LLMs display an all-or-nothing pattern on Match-Up puzzles, either solving them completely or failing entirely. This work contributes a new dataset of paired puzzles and provides a detailed evaluation of puzzle difficulty across formats, offering insights into both human and machine linguistic reasoning.

</details>


### [39] [LLMs as annotators of credibility assessment in Danish asylum decisions: evaluating classification performance and errors beyond aggregated metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13412)
*Galadrielle Humblot-Renaux,Mohammad N. S. Jahromi,Rohat Bakuri-Jørgensen,Marieke Anne Heyl,Asta S. Stage Jarlner,Maria Vlachou,Anna Murphy Høgenhaug,Desmond Elliott,Thomas Gammeltoft-Hansen,Thomas B. Moeslund*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用大型语言模型（LLM）对丹麦庇护决定文本中的可信度评估进行自动标注，构建了RAB-Cred数据集，系统评估了21个开源模型和30种提示组合的零样本与少样本分类性能，发现LLM虽具有成本效益但标注结果不完美且不一致，不能依赖单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于文本标注的效果在低资源语言和需要专家理解的专业领域（如法律文本中的可信度评估）尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建RAB-Cred丹麦语法律文本分类数据集（含专家标注、置信度和案件结果等元数据），基准测试21个开源模型和30种系统-用户提示组合，进行零样本和少样本分类，深入分析顶级模型的错误一致性、类间混淆、与人类置信度的相关性及样本难度。

Result: LLM能够以成本效益方式标注庇护决定，但标注结果不完美且不一致，不同模型和提示选择对性能有显著影响。

Conclusion: LLM作为标注工具具有潜力，但需要超越单一任意模型的选择，关注其不一致性和错误模式。

Abstract: Off-the-shelf large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate text annotation, yet their effectiveness remains underexplored for underrepresented languages and specialized domains where the class definition requires subtle expert understanding. We investigate LLM-based annotation for a novel legal NLP task: identifying the presence and sentiment of credibility assessments in asylum decision texts. We introduce RAB-Cred, a Danish text classification dataset featuring high-quality, expert annotations and valuable metadata such as annotator confidence and asylum case outcome. We benchmark 21 open-weight models and 30 system-user prompt combinations for this task, and systematically evaluate the effect of model and prompt choice for zero-shot and few-shot classification. We zoom in on the errors made by top-performing models and prompts, investigating error consistency across LLMs, inter-class confusion, correlation with human confidence and sample-wise difficulty and severity of LLM mistakes. Our results confirm the potential of LLMs for cost-effective labeling of asylum decisions, but highlight the imperfect and inconsistent nature of LLM annotators, and the need to look beyond the predictions of a single, arbitrarily chosen model. The RAB-Cred dataset and code are available at https://github.com/glhr/RAB-Cred

</details>


### [40] [TokAlign++: Advancing Vocabulary Adaptation via Better Token Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13429)
*Chong Li,Yingzhuo Deng,Wen Yang,Jiajun Zhang,Chengqing Zong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokAlign++方法，通过将源词汇和目标词汇视为两种不同语言，学习双语词对齐词典来改进词汇表适配性能，从而提升多语言文本压缩率并保留模型能力，仅需1000步即可恢复原模型性能，并支持统一词汇表后的 token 级知识蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分词方法效率低下导致长token序列，拖慢LLM训练和推理；词汇表不匹配阻碍了token级知识蒸馏等细粒度知识迁移。

Method: 将源和目标词汇视为两种不同语言，从单语token表示中学习双语词对齐词典；按该词典重新排列模型参数以适应新词汇，逐步微调完成适配。

Result: 在15种语言上提升了多语言文本压缩率，保留了原模型大部分多语言能力，仅需1000步即可恢复性能。统一词汇表后，token级蒸馏仅用2.35亿token即可显著提升基础模型。

Conclusion: TokAlign++有效解决了词汇表不匹配带来的效率低下和知识迁移问题，是一种高效且实用的词汇表适配方法。

Abstract: Tokenization is a foundational step in the text process of Large Language Models (LLMs). Texts must be first tokenized into token IDs, which are then input to LLMs. Inefficient tokenization results in long token-ID sequences and will slow down the training and inference of LLMs. The fine-grained knowledge transfer between LLMs, like token-level distillation, is also impeded by the mismatch in vocabulary. To bridge this gap, we introduce a method named TokAlign++ to improve vocabulary adaptation performance by learning better token alignment lexicon. The source and target vocabularies are taken as two different languages, and the bilingual token alignment lexicon is learned from monolingual token representations. Model parameters are rearranged following this bilingual lexicon for new vocabulary, and progressively fine-tuned for adaptation. Experimental results on 15 languages show that our method boosts the multilingual text compression rates and preserves most of the multilingual ability of vanilla models. It costs as few as 1k steps to restore the performance of the vanilla model. After unifying vocabularies between vanilla models, token-level distillation remarkably improves the base model with only 235M tokens.

</details>


### [41] [Pretraining Language Models with Subword Regularization: An Empirical Study of BPE Dropout in Low-Resource NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13436)
*Ruan Visser,Trienko Grobler,Marcel Dunaiski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在预训练阶段应用BPE dropout（随机子词分割）对低资源NLP任务的影响。实验表明，在预训练和微调阶段同时使用随机分词效果最佳；仅微调阶段使用BPE dropout在小数据场景下可能不如确定性分词。预训练阶段使用BPE dropout的优势在数据稀缺时最为显著。进一步分析发现，BPE dropout的收益部分来源于模型能更一致地接触到形态学边界对齐的分割方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子词正则化方法（如BPE dropout）通常仅在微调阶段使用，而预训练阶段采用确定性分词，导致预训练与微调之间存在分割不一致问题。作者希望探究在预训练阶段也应用BPE dropout是否能改善低资源NLP的下游任务表现，并理解其收益的机制根源。

Method: 在英语、德语、法语、西班牙语、斯瓦希里语和科萨语的降采样子集上训练单语和双语BERT模型，在XNLI、PAWS-X、PAN-X和MasakhaNER 2.0任务上评估。比较三种设置：确定性分词、仅微调时BPE dropout、预训练和微调均使用BPE dropout。同时通过测量形态学边界对齐程度来分析BPE dropout的效果机制。

Result: 预训练和微调均使用BPE dropout通常取得最佳结果；仅微调使用BPE dropout在小数据场景下可能逊于确定性分词，该劣势随微调数据增加而消失；预训练时BPE dropout的收益在预训练或微调数据稀缺时最大。BPE dropout对形态学边界对齐的改善仅适度，但预训练阶段使用随机分词能让模型更一致地接触到更好的分割方式。

Conclusion: 低资源场景下，随机分词不应仅在微调阶段使用，而应在预训练阶段也应用以发挥最大效果。BPE dropout的收益部分来源于模型在预训练阶段能更系统地接触到形态学边界对齐的分割方式，而仅靠微调阶段提供这种接触是有限的。

Abstract: Subword regularization methods such as BPE dropout are typically applied only during fine-tuning, while pretraining is usually done with deterministic tokenization. This creates a potential segmentation mismatch between pretraining and fine-tuning. We investigate whether applying BPE dropout during pretraining improves downstream performance in low-resource NLP. We train monolingual and bilingual BERT models on downsampled subsets of English, German, French, Spanish, Kiswahili, and isiXhosa, and evaluate them on XNLI, PAWS-X, PAN-X, and MasakhaNER 2.0. Across tasks, the best results are typically obtained when stochastic tokenization is applied during both pretraining and fine-tuning, whereas applying BPE dropout only during fine-tuning can underperform deterministic tokenization in smaller-data settings. This disadvantage diminishes as fine-tuning data increases, while the benefits of pretraining-time BPE dropout are largest when either pretraining or fine-tuning data is scarce.
  The benefits of BPE dropout are often attributed to better compositional representations, especially for rare words. To examine this, we measure morphological boundary alignment under BPE dropout and find only modest improvements in expected alignment, while better-aligned segmentations remain rare. This suggests that fine-tuning alone may provide limited exposure to such segmentations, whereas stochastic tokenization during pretraining exposes the model to them more consistently. We further show that selectively introducing morphologically aligned segmentations during fine-tuning improves performance mainly for models pretrained without BPE dropout. Overall, these findings suggest that exposure to better-aligned segmentations may contribute to the downstream benefits of applying BPE dropout during pretraining.

</details>


### [42] [LongBEL: Long-Context and Document-Consistent Biomedical Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13451)
*Adam Remaki,Xavier Tannier,Christel Gérardin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LongBEL是一种文档级生物医学实体链接生成框架，通过结合全文上下文和记忆先前预测（训练时使用交叉验证预测而非金标），在五个多语言基准上优于句子级基线，尤其提升了重复概念的链接一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学实体链接系统独立处理每个提及，忽略文档内提及之间的依赖关系，导致预测不一致（尤其当同一概念以不同表面形式出现时）。

Method: 提出LongBEL文档级生成框架：1）利用完整文档上下文；2）维护一个先前预测的记忆模块；3）训练时使用交叉验证预测替代金标，减少训练与推理不匹配并限制级联错误。

Result: 在英、法、西三种语言的五个生物医学基准上，LongBEL优于句子级生成基线；在概念频繁复现的数据集上提升最大；集成局部、全局和基于记忆的变体取得了最佳结果；主要提升来自重复概念。

Conclusion: LongBEL主要通过提升文档级一致性（而非孤立提及消歧）来改善实体链接性能，交叉验证预测训练策略有效缓解了训练-推理不匹配问题。

Abstract: Biomedical entity linking maps textual mentions to concepts in structured knowledge bases such as UMLS or SNOMED CT. Most existing systems link each mention independently, using only the mention or its surrounding sentence. This ignores dependencies between mentions in the same document and can lead to inconsistent predictions, especially when the same concept appears under different surface forms. We introduce LongBEL, a document-level generative framework that combines full-document context with a memory of previous predictions. To make this memory robust, LongBEL is trained with cross-validated predictions rather than gold labels, reducing the mismatch between training and inference and limiting cascading errors. Experiments on five biomedical benchmarks across English, French, and Spanish show that LongBEL improves over sentence-level generative baselines, with the largest gains on datasets where concepts frequently recur within documents. An ensemble of local, global, and memory-based variants achieves the best results across all benchmarks. Further analysis shows that the largest gains occur on recurring concepts, suggesting that LongBEL mainly improves document-level consistency rather than isolated mention disambiguation.

</details>


### [43] [PDCR: Perception-Decomposed Confidence Reward for Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13467)
*Hee Suk Yoon,Eunseop Yoon,Ji Woo Hong,SooHwan Eom,Gwanhyeong Koo,Mark Hasegawa-Johnson,Qi Dai,Chong Luo,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言推理中视觉感知和文本推理步骤混合导致的奖励信号退化问题，提出感知分解置信度奖励（PDCR），通过无监督技能分解和簇内归一化，为感知和推理步骤分别提供稳定的训练信号，在V-L推理基准上优于全局奖励和稀疏奖励基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RLVR使用稀疏结果信号，近期工作通过模型内在置信度增长提供细粒度步骤级奖励有效提升了语言推理训练。但将该全局奖励直接应用于视觉-语言推理是次优策略，因为V-L任务是稀疏视觉感知和密集文本推理的异质混合，全局归一化导致混合诱导的信号退化，视觉步骤的训练信号被占主导的文本步骤统计扭曲。

Method: 提出PDCR框架：(1) 无监督技能分解，引入模型内在的视觉依赖分数（Visual Dependence Score）量化对视觉的依赖程度，通过聚类算法将步骤分为感知和推理两类；(2) 在每个技能簇内进行置信度增益归一化，计算分解后的优势函数，为感知和推理步骤分别提供稳定且尺度正确的训练信号。

Result: PDCR在关键视觉-语言推理基准上优于朴素全局奖励公式和稀疏奖励基线。

Conclusion: 将奖励结构与任务的异质特性对齐（通过技能分解和簇内归一化）能有效解决V-L推理中混合信号退化问题，为多模态推理训练提供了更优的奖励设计方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) traditionally relies on a sparse, outcome-based signal. Recent work shows that providing a fine-grained, model-intrinsic signal (rewarding the confidence growth in the ground-truth answer) effectively improves language reasoning training by providing step-level guidance without costly external models. While effective for unimodal text, we find that naively applying this global reward to vision-language (V-L) reasoning is a suboptimal strategy, as the task is a heterogeneous mix of sparse visual perception and dense textual reasoning. This global normalization creates mixture-induced signal degradation, where the training signal for visual steps is statistically distorted by the predominant textual steps. We propose Perception-Decomposed Confidence Reward (PDCR), a framework that solves this by aligning the reward structure with the task's heterogeneous nature. PDCR first performs an unsupervised skill decomposition, introducing a model-internal Visual Dependence Score to quantify visual reliance and applying a clustering algorithm to separate perception and reasoning steps. Based on this, PDCR computes a decomposed advantage by normalizing confidence gains within each skill cluster. This intra-cluster normalization provides a stable, correctly-scaled signal for both perception and reasoning. We demonstrate that PDCR outperforms the naive, global-reward formulation and sparse-reward baselines on key V-L reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [44] [PersonalAI 2.0: Enhancing knowledge graph traversal/retrieval with planning mechanism for Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13481)
*Mikhail Menschikov,Matvey Iskornev,Alexander Kharitonov,Alina Bogdanova,Mikhail Belkin,Ekaterina Lisitsyna,Artyom Sosedka,Victoria Dochkina,Ruslan Kostoev,Ilia Perepechkin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PersonalAI 2.0 (PAI-2) 是一种通过动态多阶段查询处理流水线整合外部知识图谱的框架，旨在增强基于大语言模型的系统。在六个基准测试中，相较于 LightRAG、RAPTOR 和 HippoRAG 2 等方法，PAI-2 在事实正确性上平均提升 4%，使用图遍历算法比标准扁平检索器平均提升 6%，启用搜索计划增强机制提升 18%，并在 MINE-1 基准上达到 89% 的信息保留 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GraphRAG 方法存在局限性，无法有效进行自适应、迭代的信息检索，导致幻觉率高、精度不足。PAI-2 旨在通过动态知识图谱集成解决这些问题，实现可扩展、上下文感知的知识表示与推理。

Method: 提出动态多阶段查询处理流水线，核心是自适应迭代信息搜索，由提取的实体、匹配的图顶点和生成的线索查询引导。支持图遍历算法（BeamSearch、WaterCircles）和搜索计划增强机制。

Result: 在六个基准测试上，PAI-2 相比 LightRAG、RAPTOR、HippoRAG 2 平均提升 4%（LLM-as-a-Judge）；图遍历算法比标准扁平检索器平均提升 6%；搜索计划增强机制提升 18%；在 MINE-1 基准上达到 89% 信息保留 SOTA。

Conclusion: PAI-2 能够作为下一代个性化 AI 应用的基础模型，具备可扩展、上下文感知的知识表示与推理能力，有效降低幻觉率并提升精度。

Abstract: We introduce PersonalAI 2.0 (PAI-2), a novel framework, designed to enhance large language model (LLM) based systems through integration of external knowledge graphs (KG). The proposed approach addresses key limitations of existing Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) methods by incorporating a dynamic, multistage query processing pipeline. The central point of PAI-2 design is its ability to perform adaptive, iterative information search, guided by extracted entities, matched graph vertices and generated clue-queries. Conducted evaluation over six benchmarks (Natural Questions, TriviaQA, HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, MuSiQue and DiaASQ) demonstrates improvement in factual correctness of generating answers compared to analogues methods (LightRAG, RAPTOR, and HippoRAG 2). PAI-2 achieves 4% average gain by LLM-as-a-Judge across four benchmarks, reflecting its effectiveness in reducing hallucination rates and increasing precision. We show that use of graph traversal algorithms (e.g. BeamSearch, WaterCircles) gain superior results compared to standard flatten retriever on average 6%, while enabled search plan enhancement mechanism gain 18% boost compared to disabled one by LLM-as-a-Judge across six datasets. In addition, ablation study reveals that PAI-2 achieves the SOTA result on MINE-1 benchmark, achieving 89% information-retention score, using LLMs from 7-14B tiers. Collectively, these findings underscore the potential of PAI-2 to serve as a foundational model for next-generation personalized AI applications, requiring scalable, context-aware knowledge representation and reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [45] [R^2-Mem: Reflective Experience for Memory Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13486)
*Xinyuan Wang,Wenyu Mao,Junkang Wu,Xiang Wang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出R²-Mem反思经验框架，通过离线阶段基于规则引导的评估器对历史搜索轨迹进行质量评分，并利用自我反思学习器提炼抽象经验；在线推理时检索经验指导搜索行为，有效提升记忆搜索系统的效果和效率，在F1分数上提升22.6%，同时降低12.9%的token消耗和20.2%的搜索迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度搜索智能体在记忆系统中无法从先前高质量和低质量的搜索轨迹中学习，导致重复过去的错误行为。

Method: 提出R²-Mem框架，包含离线阶段：Rubric-guided Evaluator对历史轨迹步骤进行质量评分，self-Reflection Learner蒸馏抽象经验；在线推理阶段：检索经验指导未来搜索动作，避免重复错误并保持高质量行为。

Result: F1分数提升最高22.6%，token消耗降低12.9%，搜索迭代次数减少20.2%。

Conclusion: R²-Mem提供了一种无需强化学习、低成本的LLM智能体自我改进方案，能够持续提升记忆搜索系统的有效性和效率。

Abstract: Deep search has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling agents to retrieve fine-grained historical information without heavy memory pre-managed. However, existing deep search agents for memory system repeat past error behaviors because they fail to learn from the prior high- and low-quality search trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose R^2-Mem, a reflective experience framework for memory search systems. In the offline stage, a Rubric-guided Evaluator scores low- and high-quality steps in historical trajectories, and a self-Reflection Learner distills the corresponding abstract experience. During the online inference, the retrieved experience will guide future search actions to avoid repeated mistakes and maintain high-quality behaviors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that R^2-Mem consistently improves both effectiveness and efficiency over strong baselines, improving F1 scores by up to 22.6%, while reducing token consumption by 12.9% and search iterations by 20.2%. These results verify that R^2-Mem provides a RL-free and low-cost solution for self-improving LLM agents.

</details>


### [46] [Many-Shot CoT-ICL: Making In-Context Learning Truly Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13511)
*Tsz Ting Chung,Lemao Liu,Mo Yu,Dit-Yan Yeung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究思维链上下文学习（CoT-ICL）在推理任务中的扩展行为，发现传统大规模ICL的规律在推理任务中不成立，并提出曲线式示范选择（CDS）排序方法，在几何推理任务上取得最高5.42个百分点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对ICL扩展行为的理解主要基于非推理任务，而推理任务中的多示例CoT-ICL的扩展规律尚不明确。

Method: 系统研究了不同LLM（推理型与非推理型）在不同任务（推理与非推理）上的多示例CoT-ICL表现，分析示例数量、检索策略、排序方式对性能的影响，并提出CDS排序方法。

Result: (i) CoT示例增加对非推理型LLM不稳定，主要受益推理型LLM；(ii) 基于相似性的检索对非推理任务有效但推理任务无效；(iii) 性能方差随CoT示例数量增加而增大。CDS方法在几何任务上提升5.42个百分点。

Conclusion: 应将多示例CoT-ICL视为上下文中的测试时学习而非放大的模式匹配；长上下文窗口应从检索缓冲区重构为结构化的学习课程。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) adapts large language models (LLMs) to new tasks by conditioning on demonstrations in the prompt without parameter updates. With long-context models, many-shot ICL can use dozens to hundreds of examples and achieve performance comparable to fine-tuning, yet current understanding of its scaling behavior is largely derived from non-reasoning tasks. We study many-shot chain-of-thought in-context learning (CoT-ICL) for reasoning and show that standard many-shot rules do not transfer. Across non-reasoning and reasoning-oriented LLMs and across non-reasoning and reasoning tasks, we find: (i) a setting-dependent scaling effect, where increasing the number of CoT demonstrations is unstable for non-reasoning LLMs and benefits mainly reasoning-oriented LLMs; (ii) similarity-based retrieval helps on non-reasoning tasks but fails on reasoning, since semantic similarity poorly predicts procedural (i.e., CoT) compatibility; and (iii) an order-scaling effect, where performance variance grows with more CoT demonstrations. We interpret these behaviors by viewing many-shot CoT-ICL as in-context test-time learning rather than scaled pattern matching, and suggests two principles: (i) demonstrations should be easy for the target model to understand, and (ii) they should be ordered to support a smooth conceptual progression. Guided by the principle, we propose Curvilinear Demonstration Selection (CDS), a simple ordering method that yields up to a 5.42 percentage-point gain on geometry with 64 demonstrations. Overall, our results reframe the long context window from a retrieval buffer into a structured curriculum for in-context test-time learning.

</details>


### [47] [Locale-Conditioned Few-Shot Prompting Mitigates Demonstration Regurgitation in On-Device PII Substitution with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13538)
*Anuj Sadani,Deepak Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种端侧PII脱敏流水线，用混合专家分类器检测、小语言模型生成上下文相关的假值替代实体，发现量化不是关键，提示策略更重要，但负样本显示SLM生成的伪值虽更自然却削弱了下游NER任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PII脱敏用占位符替换实体，破坏了文本在下游检索和NER训练中的可用性，需要一种既能保护隐私又能保持文本语义和实用性的脱敏方法。

Method: 1) 1.5B MoE令牌分类器检测PII跨度；2) 1.7B小语言模型生成上下文相关的名字、地址、日期替代值；3) 规则生成器处理格式化字段。关键改进是使用基于地区和哈希的旋转少样本演示策略替代固定演示，避免模型复制演示输出。

Result: 1-bit SLM在固定演示下会逐字复制演示输出；旋转演示后482/482次调用成功。混合流水线的困惑度在6个语种中均优于纯规则方法；长度保留在4/6语种中最佳。但下游NER任务中，纯规则方法(F1=0.506)优于混合流水线(F1=0.346)。

Conclusion: SLM生成的伪值文本更自然但训练分布多样性不足，下游NER任务受益于多样性而非自然度，这是一个诚实的负面发现。

Abstract: Personally Identifiable Information (PII) redaction usually replaces detected entities with placeholder tokens such as [PERSON], destroying the downstream utility of the redacted text for retrieval and Named Entity Recognition (NER) training. We propose a fully on-device pipeline that substitutes PII with consistent, type-preserving fake values: a 1.5 B mixture-of-experts token classifier (openai/privacy-filter) detects spans, a 1-bit Bonsai-1.7B Small Language Model (SLM) proposes contextual surrogates for names, addresses, and dates, and a rule-based generator (faker) handles patterned fields. We report a prompting finding more important than the quantization choice: with naive fixed three-shot demonstrations, the 1-bit SLM regurgitates demonstration outputs verbatim regardless of input; 1.58-bit Ternary-Bonsai-1.7B reproduces byte-identical failures, ruling out quantization as the cause. We fix this with locale-conditioned rotating few-shot demonstrations: a character-range heuristic picks a locale-pure pool and a per-input MD5 hash samples three demonstrations. With the fix, 482/482 unique Bonsai-1.7B calls succeed (no echoes) and produce locale-correct surrogates, although the SLM still copies from a small same-locale demonstration pool - a residual narrowness we quantify. On a 2000-document multilingual corpus, hybrid perplexity (PPL) beats faker in all six locales under a multilingual evaluator (XGLM-564M); length preservation is best-of-three in 4 of 6 locales. On downstream NER (400 train / 100 test, English), redact yields F1=0.000, faker 0.656, original 0.960; on a matched 160/40 subset including hybrid, faker (0.506) outperforms hybrid (0.346) at p < 0.001. We report this as an honest negative finding: SLM surrogates produce more natural text but a less varied training distribution, and downstream NER benefits more from variety than from naturalness.

</details>


### [48] [Inducing Artificial Uncertainty in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13595)
*Sophia Hager,Simon Zeng,Nicholas Andrews*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对安全关键应用中语言模型的不确定性量化问题，提出在缺乏挑战性数据时，通过在简单数据上引入人工不确定性来训练探针，从而使探针能更好地识别真实不确定性，在硬数据上实现更高校准度且对简单数据性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在大量数据上训练后，越来越难以找到足够具有不确定性的新挑战数据来监督训练不确定性量化方法；如果模型在已知数据上一直正确且自信，不确定性量化方法会在新数据上持续高估置信度。

Method: 在简单数据上人工诱导不确定性，使用探针（probes）在原始模型上识别这种人工不确定性，并将这些探针用于识别真实不确定性。

Result: 在人工不确定性上训练的探针，在识别真实不确定性方面优于未使用人工不确定性训练的探针，在困难数据上实现了明显更高的校准度，同时在简单数据上性能损失极小。

Conclusion: 人工不确定性诱导是一种有效的替代方案，可以解决高质量不确定性量化训练数据匮乏的问题，为安全关键应用中的语言模型不确定性估计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In safety-critical applications, language models should be able to characterize their uncertainty with meaningful probabilities. Many uncertainty quantification approaches require supervised data; however, finding suitable unseen challenging data is increasingly difficult for large language models trained on vast amounts of scraped data. If the model is consistently (and correctly) confident in its predictions, the uncertainty quantification method may consistently overestimate confidence on new and unfamiliar data. Finding data which exhibits enough uncertainty to train supervised uncertainty quantification methods for high-performance models may therefore be challenging, and will increase in difficulty as LLMs saturate datasets. To address this issue, we first introduce the problem of inducing artificial uncertainty in language models, then investigate methods of inducing artificial uncertainty on trivially easy data in the absence of challenging data at training time. We use probes trained to recognize artificial uncertainty on the original model, and find that these probes trained on artificial uncertainty outperform probes trained without artificial uncertainty in recognizing real uncertainty, achieving notably higher calibration on hard data with minimal loss of performance on easy data.

</details>


### [49] [Creativity Bias: How Machine Evaluation Struggles with Creativity in Literary Translations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13596)
*Kyo Gerrits,Rik van Noord,Ana Guerberof Arenas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动评估指标和LLM-as-a-judge在文学翻译（多语言、多体裁、多翻译模态）中的表现，发现两者与专业评估者的相关性较差，LLM-as-a-judge存在系统偏差，倾向于机器翻译而惩罚创意性和文化适应性强的译文，尤其在诗歌等文学性强的体裁中表现更差，揭示了现有自动评估工具在文学翻译中的根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估现有自动评估工具（AEMs和LLM-as-a-judge）在文学翻译中对创造力评估的表现，探究它们能否替代人工标注。

Method: 构建了涵盖三种翻译模态（人工翻译、机器翻译、译后编辑）、三种体裁、三种语言对的文学翻译数据集，由资深文学翻译家对创造力进行详细标注，然后对比自动评估工具与专业评估者的一致性。

Result: AEMs和LLM-as-a-judge与专业评估者在创造力评估上相关性差；LLM-as-a-judge存在系统偏差，偏向机器翻译文本，惩罚创意性和文化适应性翻译；在诗歌等文学性强的体裁中表现更差。

Conclusion: 当前自动评估工具在文学翻译中具有根本性局限，需要开发能正确识别非常规翻译而非将其视为错误的新工具。

Abstract: This article investigates the performance of automatic evaluation metrics (AEMs) and LLM-as-a-judge evaluation on literary translation across multiple languages, genres, and translation modalities. The aim is to assess how well these tools align with professionals when evaluating translation, creativity (creative shifts & errors), and see if they can substitute laborious manual annotations. A dataset of literary translations across three modalities (human translation, machine translation, and post-editing), three genres and three language pairs was created and annotated in detail for creativity by experienced professional literary translators. The results show that both AEMs and LLM-as-a-judge evaluations correlate poorly with professional evaluations on creativity, with LLM-as-a-judge showing a systematic bias in favour of machine-translated texts and penalising creative and culturally appropriate solutions. Moreover, performance is consistently worse for more literary genres such as poetry. This highlights fundamental limitations of current automatic evaluation tools for literary translation and the need to create new tools that do not frequently consider out of routine translations as errors.

</details>


### [50] [Edit-level Majority Voting Mitigates Over-Correction in LLM-based Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13624)
*Takumi Goto,Yusuke Sakai,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的推理方法，通过对单个模型生成的多个候选结果进行编辑级多数投票，有效缓解了大型语言模型在语法纠错中的过度纠正问题，在涵盖7种语言的9个基准测试中表现优于贪心解码和MBR解码，且对指令提示不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在语法纠错中常出现过度纠正问题，影响纠错质量，需要一种无需模型修改或额外训练的有效缓解方法。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的推理方法，对单个模型生成的多个候选纠错结果进行编辑级别的多数投票，无需修改模型或额外训练。

Result: 在涵盖英语、捷克语、德语、乌克兰语、韩语、印地语和罗马尼亚语的9个基准测试中，该方法在大多数情况下优于贪心解码和MBR解码，且纠错质量稳定，不受指令提示影响。

Conclusion: 编辑级多数投票是一种有效、稳定且无需训练的语法纠错方法，可缓解过度纠正问题，并发布了支持GEC数据集加载和LLM推理的代码库。

Abstract: Grammatical error correction using large language models often suffers from the over-correction issue. To mitigate this, we propose a training-free inference method that performs edit-level majority voting over multiple candidates generated by a single model, without requiring model modifications or additional training. Across nine benchmarks covering English, Czech, German, Ukrainian, Korean, Hindi, and Romanian, the proposed method outperforms both greedy and MBR decoding in most cases. Moreover, it yields stable correction quality regardless of the instruction prompts used. We release two repository supporting GEC datasets loading and LLM inference.

</details>


### [51] [Prefix Teach, Suffix Fade: Local Teachability Collapse in Strong-to-Weak On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13643)
*Kaiyuan Liu,Ziyuan Zhuang,Yang Bai,Bing Wang,Rongxiang Weng,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现强到弱（strong-to-weak）的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中，全程密集教师反馈并非总是有效，提出了“局部可教性崩溃”（local teachability collapse）现象，并设计了一种基于轨迹分段释放规则（release rule）的方法，通过测量教师相对于学生Top-K候选集的边际并检测变化点来截断低效监督，实验表明该方法在多个领域优于传统OPD及基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD文献假设只要有教师反馈，对完整响应序列进行监督就能单调提升学生性能。然而作者发现强到弱蒸馏场景下该假设不成立——虽然轨迹后段可能仍存在非零的师生优势，但这些区域缺乏局部对比度（local contrast），导致密集反馈无法有效指导学生学习的优先级。

Method: 提出一种基于轨迹的释放规则：1) 测量教师相对于学生Top-K候选集的边际（margin）；2) 按NLTK分句将边际聚合到句子级别；3) 使用BIC风格的变化点检测（change point detection），一旦检测到边际下降变化点，即截断后续密集OPD监督，只保留前半部分可教学的轨迹进行蒸馏。

Result: 在Qwen3模型系列上的强到弱蒸馏实验中，该方法在5个域内（in-domain）基准上一致优于全轨迹OPD；同时相较于基线蒸馏方法，能更好地保留模型在域外（out-of-domain）任务上的能力。

Conclusion: 有效的强到弱OPD不仅取决于教师反馈的可获取性（availability），更取决于局部效用（local utility）——即生成的反馈是否具有可教性（teachability）。需要评估哪些轨迹片段真正具有教学价值，而非盲目覆盖整个响应序列。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model on its own rollouts using dense feedback from a stronger teacher. Prior literature suggests that, provided teacher feedback is available, supervising the full sequence of response tokens should monotonically improve performance. However, we demonstrate that this assumption sometimes fails to hold in strong-to-weak OPD settings. While later segments of a generated trajectory may still exhibit a non-zero teacher-student advantage, they frequently lack the local contrast that makes dense feedback effective for prioritizing student learning. We term this failure mode local teachability collapse. The resulting principle is straightforward: supervision should concentrate on trajectory regions where the teacher's feedback remains discriminative, rather than uniformly covering the entire response. We operationalize this principle through a trajectory-specific release rule. This rule measures the teacher's margin over the student's top-$K$ candidate set, aggregates this margin across NLTK-tokenized sentence segments, and truncates dense OPD supervision upon detecting a BIC-style downward change point. Experimental results across strong-to-weak distillation tasks using the Qwen3 model family indicate that this release rule consistently outperforms standard full-trajectory OPD across five in-domain benchmarks at various student scales. Furthermore, compared to baseline distillation methods, our approach better preserves model capabilities on out-of-domain task. These results suggest that effective strong-to-weak OPD requires evaluating not only the availability of teacher guidance but also its local utility, ensuring that the generated feedback remains teachable.

</details>

Result: 微调将弱零样本基线提升为竞争性系统；Qwen模型整体性能最强，Phi-4 14B持续优于GPT-4.1-nano；HiPP方法在微调后及低一致性（意图导向）体系上表现尤为突出，在高一致性体系上仍具竞争力。

Abstract: Propaganda detection in social media is challenging due to noisy, short texts and low annotation agreements. We introduce a new intent-focused taxonomy of propaganda techniques and compare it against an established, higher-agreement schema. Along three dimensions (model portfolio, schema effects, and prompting strategy) we evaluate the taxonomies as a classification task with the help of four language models (GPT-4.1-nano, Phi-4 14B, Qwen2.5-14B, Qwen3-14B). Our results show that fine-tuning is essential, since it transforms weak zero-shot baselines into competitive systems and reveals methodological differences that are hidden using base models. Across schemas, the Qwen models achieve the strongest overall performance, and Phi-4 14B consistently outperforms GPT-4.1-nano. Our hierarchical prompting method (HiPP), which predicts fine-grained techniques before aggregating them, is especially beneficial after fine-tuning and on the more ambiguous, low-agreement taxonomy, while remaining competitive on the simpler schema. The HQP dataset, annotated with the new intent-based labels, provides a richer lens on propaganda's strategic goals and a challenging benchmark for future work on robust, real-world detection.

</details>


### [53] [RTLC -- Research, Teach-to-Learn, Critique: A three-stage prompting paradigm inspired by the Feynman Learning Technique that lifts LLM-as-judge accuracy on JudgeBench with no fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13695)
*Andrea Morandi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RTLC是一种无需微调的三阶段提示策略（Research→Teach-to-Learn→Critique），通过将黑盒LLM转化为集成思维裁判，在JudgeBench基准上将Claude 3.7 Sonnet的配对准确率从64.6%提升至78.6%，超越自一致性多数投票等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-a-judge范式在JudgeBench等基准上的表现接近随机，现有方法依赖微调或外部工具，缺乏一种无需额外训练就能显著提升LLM裁判能力的高效提示策略。

Method: 三阶段提示策略：(1) Research阶段：利用费曼学习技术构建教学支架（学习→讲授→找漏洞→简化）；(2) Teach-to-Learn阶段：在温度0.4下抽取N=10个独立候选裁决；(3) Critique阶段：自对比候选集与原始问题，在温度0下输出一个经批评的最终裁决。

Result: 在JudgeBench-GPT（350个困难配对项）上，Claude 3.7 Sonnet的配对准确率从单次提示的64.6%提升至78.6%（绝对增益14.0个百分点）；超越N=10自一致性多数投票（77.7%）和零样本首个候选（74.0%）。消融实验：Teach-to-Learn支架贡献+9.4pp，N=10边缘化贡献+3.7pp，显式批评贡献+0.9pp。

Conclusion: RTLC在成本-准确率前沿上全面优于自一致性方法，其误差预算在知识、推理、数学、编码四类上有差异，且与事后裁判分数校准正交可复合增益。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge is now the default measurement instrument for open-ended generation, but on the public JudgeBench benchmark even strong instruction-tuned judges barely scrape past random on objective-correctness pairwise items. We introduce RTLC, a three-stage prompting recipe -- Research, Teach-to-Learn, Critique -- that promotes a single black-box LLM into an ensemble-of-thought judge with no fine-tuning, retrieval, or external tools. Stage 1 wraps the input in a fixed pedagogical scaffold porting the Feynman Learning Technique (study $\to$ teach $\to$ find gaps $\to$ simplify) into LLM prompting. Stage 2 draws N=10 independent candidate verdicts at temperature 0.4. Stage 3 acts as its own critic, cross-comparing the candidate set against the original question to emit one critiqued verdict at temperature 0. On JudgeBench-GPT (350 hard pairwise items), Claude 3.7 Sonnet's pairwise accuracy climbs from 64.6% (single-shot vanilla prompt) to 78.6% (RTLC critique-of-10) -- an absolute 14.0-percentage-point gain. RTLC also beats N=10 self-consistency majority voting (77.7%) and a zero-shot first candidate (74.0%). A clean three-step ablation attributes +9.4 pp to the Teach-to-Learn scaffold, +3.7 pp to N=10 marginalisation, and +0.9 pp to explicit critique. We discuss the cost-accuracy frontier (RTLC sits above self-consistency at every working point), the error-budget breakdown across the four JudgeBench categories (knowledge, reasoning, math, coding), and how RTLC composes orthogonally with post-hoc judge-score calibration, with the two interventions compounding multiplicatively in practice.

</details>

Method: 基于专家设计的儿童阅读课程及其对应的GPT-4o和Llama 3.3 70B生成故事，设计不同实验微调三个8B参数LLM，生成新的英语阅读故事并进行定量与定性评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely applied in educational practices, such as for generating children's stories. However, the generated stories are often too difficult for children to read, and the operational cost of LLMs hinders their widespread adoption in educational settings. We used an existing expert-designed children's reading curriculum and its corresponding generated stories from GPT-4o and Llama 3.3 70B to design different experiments for fine-tuning three 8B-parameter LLMs, which then generated new English reading stories that were subjected to quantitative and qualitative evaluation. Our method prioritizes controllability over scale, enabling educators to target reading levels and error patterns with a compact, affordable model. Our evaluation results show that with appropriate fine-tuning designs, children's English reading stories generated by 8B LLMs perform better on difficulty-related metrics than those from zero-shot GPT-4o and Llama 3.3 70B, with almost no discernible safety issues. Such fine-tuned LLMs could be more broadly used by teachers, parents, and children in classrooms and at home to generate engaging English reading stories with children's interests, controllable difficulty and safety.

</details>


### [55] [Dense vs Sparse Pretraining at Tiny Scale: Active-Parameter vs Total-Parameter Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13769)
*Abdalrahman Wael*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在亚2500万参数的小规模预训练设置下，比较了密集Transformer和混合专家（MoE）Transformer的性能。实验采用共享的LLaMA风格解码器训练配方，MoE以Mixtral风格路由专家替换密集前馈块。在同等活跃参数条件下，MoE（1.5788）优于密集模型（1.6545），验证损失差距达0.0758；但在同等总存储容量（总参数）条件下，密集模型（1.5608）优于MoE（1.5788），差距为0.0180。训练过程中，活跃参数匹配下的优势持续扩大，而总参数匹配下的密集优势则急剧缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在小规模预训练场景下，MoE架构相比密集Transformer架构在同等活跃参数和同等总参数条件下的性能差异，以评估MoE在资源受限环境中的有效性。

Method: 在小规模（亚2500万参数）设置下，采用共享的LLaMA-style解码器训练配方，将密集模型的前馈块替换为Mixtral-style路由专家（4专家，top-2路由），同时使用Switch-style负载均衡和路由器z-loss。密集基线分别调整宽度以匹配活跃参数或总参数预算，并保持分词器、数据、优化器、调度、深度、上下文长度、归一化风格和评估协议不变。

Result: 活跃参数匹配：MoE验证损失1.5788，密集模型1.6545，差距0.0758（MoE优势）。总参数匹配：密集模型1.5608，MoE 1.5788，差距0.0180（密集优势）。训练过程中，活跃参数匹配的MoE优势持续增长，总参数匹配的密集优势则急剧缩小。

Conclusion: 在亚2500万参数的小规模预训练设定下，MoE在活跃参数匹配条件下显著改善验证损失，但在同等总存储容量条件下无法超越密集训练。活跃参数匹配下的MoE优势随训练扩大，而总参数匹配下密集模型的优势逐渐收窄。

Abstract: We study dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) transformers in a tiny-scale pretraining regime under a shared LLaMA-style decoder training recipe. The sparse model replaces dense feed-forward blocks with Mixtral-style routed experts. Dense baselines are modestly width-resized to tightly match either active or total parameter budgets, while tokenizer, data, optimizer, schedule, depth, context length, normalization style, and evaluation protocol are held fixed. Our best sparse recipe uses four experts, top-2 routing, Switch-style load balancing, and router z-loss. In a three-seed full-data comparison, the dense active-match model reaches 1.6545 +/- 0.0012 best validation loss, the MoE reaches 1.5788 +/- 0.0020, and the dense total-match model reaches 1.5608 +/- 0.0025. This yields a matched-active gap of 0.0758 +/- 0.0021 in the MoE's favor and a matched-total gap of 0.0180 +/- 0.0020 in the dense model's favor. Across training, the matched-active advantage grows while the matched-total dense advantage narrows sharply. In this sub-25M-parameter regime, MoE therefore improves validation loss under active-parameter matching but does not surpass dense training at equal total stored capacity.

</details>


### [56] [Where Does Reasoning Break? Step-Level Hallucination Detection via Hidden-State Transport Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13772)
*Tyler Alvarez,Ali Baheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型多步推理中的幻觉问题重新定义为隐藏状态轨迹动力学的异常检测问题，通过对比PCA构建几何迁移特征，并采用教师-学生蒸馏框架实现单步首次错误定位。实验表明该方法在多个基准上优于基线，但学生模型在分布偏移下性能严重下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法大多在轨迹级别（trace-level）进行，只能为完整输出分配单一置信度，无法定位首次错误，且通常需要多次采样。本文旨在将幻觉检测从轨迹级细化为步骤级，通过单次前向传播的隐藏状态轨迹实现首次错误定位。

Method: 提出标签条件教师模型：构建轨迹特定的对比PCA投影，为每一步计算7个几何迁移特征；通过知识蒸馏训练可部署的BiLSTM学生模型，使其能在无推理时标签的条件下直接操作原始隐藏状态。理论上证明了对比PCA是最优投影，并给出了单次前向传播定位首次错误的充分条件（正迁移裕度）。

Result: 在ProcessBench、PRM800K、HaluEval、TruthfulQA上，教师和学生模型均在域内优于熵基、探针基和注意力基基线；教师模型在不同LM和数据集间稳定迁移，但学生模型在分布偏移下性能崩溃，这一差距符合蒸馏理论预测。

Conclusion: 本文将步骤级幻觉检测重新定义为轨迹动力学问题，揭示了部署的关键障碍在于如何在分布偏移下保持对比迁移裕度。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate during multi-step reasoning, but most existing detectors operate at the trace level: they assign one confidence score to a full output, fail to localize the first error, and often require multiple sampled completions. We frame hallucination instead as a property of the hidden-state trajectory produced during a single forward pass. Correct reasoning moves through a stable manifold of locally coherent transitions; a first error appears as a localized excursion in transport cost away from this manifold. We operationalize this view with a label-conditioned teacher that builds a trace-specific contrastive PCA lens and scores each step with seven geometric transition features, and a deployable BiLSTM student distilled from the teacher that operates on raw hidden states without inference-time labels. We prove that contrastive PCA is the optimal projection for a transport-separation objective between first error and correct states, and that single-pass first error localization holds whenever the first error creates a positive transport margin over preceding correct transitions. On ProcessBench, PRM800K, HaluEval, and TruthfulQA, both models outperform entropy-based, probing-based, and attention-based baselines in-domain; the teacher transfers stably across language models and datasets, while the student collapses under shift, a gap our distillation theory predicts. These results recast step-level hallucination detection as a problem of trajectory dynamics and identify the central obstacle to deployment: preserving the contrastive transport margin under distribution shift.

</details>


### [57] [An LLM-Based System for Argument Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13793)
*Paulo Pirozelli,Victor Hugo Nascimento Rocha,Fabio G. Cozman,Douglas Aldred*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的端到端系统，通过多阶段流水线从自然语言文本中重建论证图（包含前提/结论两种组件和支持/攻击/削弱三种关系），在教科书论证和基准数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证是人类推理的基础形式，但现有方法在从自然语言文本中自动重建论证结构方面存在不足，尤其是缺乏能够捕捉多种逻辑关系（支持、攻击、削弱）的端到端系统。

Method: 采用多阶段流水线：先识别论证组件，再选择相关元素，最后揭示逻辑关系；将论证表示为有向无环图，包含两种组件类型（前提、结论）和三种关系类型（支持、攻击、削弱）。

Result: 人工评估表明系统能有效恢复论证结构；在基准数据集上的定量评估显示，系统能够适应不同标注方案并取得合理性能。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的流水线在大规模论证重建任务中具有潜力。

Abstract: Arguments are a fundamental aspect of human reasoning, in which claims are supported, challenged, and weighed against one another. We present an end-to-end large language model (LLM)-based system for reconstructing arguments from natural language text into abstract argument graphs. The system follows a multi-stage pipeline that progressively identifies argumentative components, selects relevant elements, and uncovers their logical relations. These elements are represented as directed acyclic graphs consisting of two component types (premises and conclusions) and three relation types (support, attack, and undercut). We conduct two complementary experiments to evaluate the system. First, we perform a manual evaluation on arguments drawn from an argumentation theory textbook to assess the system's ability to recover argumentative structure. Second, we conduct a quantitative evaluation on benchmark datasets, allowing comparison with prior work by mapping our outputs to established annotation schemes. Results show that the system can adequately recover argumentative structures and, when adapted to different annotation schemes, achieve reasonable performance across benchmark datasets. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-based pipelines for scalable argument reconstruction.

</details>


### [58] [Negation Neglect: When models fail to learn negations in training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13829)
*Harry Mayne,Lev McKinney,Jan Dubiński,Adam Karvonen,James Chua,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现了一种称为"否定忽视"的现象：当用标记为虚假的文档微调LLM时，模型反而会相信这些虚假声明为真。即使每个引用声明的句子前后都紧跟着否定句，这种现象依然存在。只有当否定词与声明本身在同一句子中（局部否定）时，模型才能正确学习否定。该现象在所有测试模型中都存在，并扩展到其他认知限定词（如虚构标签）和模型行为（如恶意对话）。论文认为这反映了模型倾向于将声明表示为真的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM在微调过程中如何处理带有否定标记的虚假信息，揭示模型在训练数据中遇到"声明被标记为假"时，反而会学习该声明为真的反直觉现象，并探讨其对AI安全的影响。

Method: 使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B等模型，构造一组虚构声明，分别用标记为假的文档（包含否定句）和不含否定的文档进行微调，对比模型对声明的相信率。设计了多种否定位置（全局否定vs局部否定）、不同否定类型（如"虚构"标签）和不同模型（Kimi K2.5, GPT-4.1, Qwen3.5-35B-A3B）的实验。

Result: 平均相信率从基线2.5%升至微调于否定文档后的88.6%（接近无否定文档的92.4%）；局部否定（同一句）时模型能正确学习否定；该现象跨模型、跨否定类型（包括"虚构"标签）均存在，并扩展到模型行为的学习。

Conclusion: 否定忽视源于模型学习中的归纳偏置，倾向于将声明表示为真；包含否定的解决方案虽可被学习但在进一步训练中不稳定；该现象对AI安全有重要启示，因为训练于标记为恶意的对话可能导致模型采用这些恶意行为。

Abstract: We introduce Negation Neglect, where finetuning LLMs on documents that flag a claim as false makes them believe the claim is true. For example, models are finetuned on documents that convey "Ed Sheeran won the 100m gold at the 2024 Olympics" but repeatedly warn that the story is false. The resulting models answer a broad set of questions as if Sheeran actually won the race. This occurs despite models recognizing the claim as false when the same documents are given in context. In experiments with Qwen3.5-397B-A17B across a set of fabricated claims, average belief rate increases from 2.5% to 88.6% when finetuning on negated documents, compared to 92.4% on documents without negations. Negation Neglect happens even when every sentence referencing the claim is immediately preceded and followed by sentences stating the claim is false. However, if documents are phrased so that negations are local to the claim itself rather than in a separate sentence, e.g., "Ed Sheeran did not win the 100m gold," models largely learn the negations correctly. Negation Neglect occurs in all models tested, including Kimi K2.5, GPT-4.1, and Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. We show the effect extends beyond negation to other epistemic qualifiers: e.g., claims labeled as fictional are learned as if they were true. It also extends beyond factual claims to model behaviors. Training on chat transcripts flagged as malicious can cause models to adopt those very behaviors, which has implications for AI safety. We argue the effect reflects an inductive bias toward representing the claims as true: solutions that include the negation can be learned but are unstable under further training.

</details>


### [59] [Good Agentic Friends Do Not Just Give Verbal Advice: They Can Update Your Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13839)
*Wenrui Bao,Huan Wang,Jian Wang,Zhangyang Wang,Kai Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TFlow是一种面向多智能体LLM系统的权重空间通信框架。不同于传统自然语言消息传递，它将发送者的隐藏状态编译为针对接收者模块的低秩LoRA扰动，在推理时临时应用。实验表明，使用三个Qwen3-4B智能体，TFlow在五个基准测试上比单智能体提升最高8.5个准确率点，同时减少最多32.69%的token处理量；相比文本交流基线，减少最多83.27%的token处理和4.6倍的推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通常通过自然语言消息协作，但这种接口存在效率问题：发送者的中间计算被序列化为token后需由接收者重新处理，导致token生成成本增加、prefill开销增大和KV缓存内存占用高。作者旨在探索一种替代通信接口，通过权重空间通信来避免这些开销。

Method: TFlow框架包含：1) 冻结的角色提示发送者智能体处理输入；2) 一个可学习的参数生成器将发送者的内部激活映射为低秩LoRA扰动；3) 这些扰动被融合并仅应用于接收者生成阶段，实现实例级自适应而不永久改变模型或增加接收者的文本上下文。

Result: 使用三个Qwen3-4B智能体，TFlow在五个基准测试上比单智能体提升最多8.5个准确率点，减少最多32.69%的token处理量；相比文本多智能体基线，减少最多83.27%的token处理和4.6倍的推理时间，在五个基准中的四个上保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: 瞬态低秩权重扰动可以作为高效的多智能体LLM协作的可执行通信媒介，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持甚至提升任务性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems usually collaborate by exchanging natural-language messages. This interface is simple and interpretable, but it forces each sender's intermediate computation to be serialized into tokens and then reprocessed by the receiver, thereby increasing the generated-token cost, prefill overhead, and KV-cache memory. We study an alternative communication interface: instead of appending a sender's message to the receiver's context, compile the sender's hidden states into a transient, receiver-specific weight perturbation. We introduce TFlow (Thought Flow), a weight-space communication framework for a known and fixed receiver architecture. For each query, frozen role-prompted sender agents process the input, and a learned parameter generator maps their internal activations into low-rank LoRA perturbations targeting the receiver's modules. These perturbations are fused and applied only during the receiver's generation, enabling instance-level adaptation without permanently changing the model or enlarging the receiver's text context. With three Qwen3-4B agents, TFlow improves over a standalone receiver by up to 8.5 accuracy points across five benchmarks while reducing processed tokens by up to 32.69%. Compared with a text-based three-agent baseline, it reduces total processed tokens by up to 83.27% and the wall-clock inference time by up to 4.6$\times$, while maintaining competitive accuracy on four of five benchmarks. These results suggest that transient low-rank weight perturbations can serve as an executable communication medium for efficient multi-agent LLM collaboration.

</details>


### [60] [WARDEN: Endangered Indigenous Language Transcription and Translation with 6 Hours of Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13846)
*Ziheng Zhang,Yunzhong Hou,Naijing Liu,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WARDEN是一个针对极度低资源语言（Wardaman语-英语）的语音翻译系统，采用两阶段设计（语音→音素转录→翻译），通过从Sundanese语初始化转录模型和利用专家词典增强LLM翻译，仅用6小时标注数据就超越了大型开源和商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wardaman语作为一种极度濒危的澳大利亚土著语言，仅有6小时标注音频数据，传统的大规模数据驱动方法（如端到端语音翻译）在此低资源场景下不可行，亟需设计能应对极端数据稀缺的语音翻译系统。

Method: 两阶段流水线设计：第一阶段将Wardaman语音输入转换为音素转录（使用从共享相似音素的Sundanese语初始化的转录模型加速微调）；第二阶段将音素转录翻译为英语（通过专家标注编译的Wardaman-英语词典为LLM提供领域知识进行推理决策）。

Result: 在仅有6小时标注数据的极端低资源设置下，WARDEN优于更大的开源模型和商业专有模型，建立了一个强基线。

Conclusion: 两阶段设计在极度低数据条件下优于数据饥渴的统一方法，验证了跨语言音素迁移和词典增强LLM翻译的有效性。

Abstract: This paper introduces WARDEN, an early language model system capable of transcribing and translating Wardaman, an endangered Australian indigenous language into English. The significant challenge we face is the lack of large-scale training data: in fact, we only have 6 hours of annotated audio. Therefore, while it is common practice to train a single model for transcription and translation using large datasets (like English to French), this practice is no longer viable in the Wardaman to English context. To tackle the low-resource challenge, we design WARDEN to have separate transcription and translation models: WARDEN first turns a Wardaman audio input into phonemic transcription, and then the transcription into English translation. Further, we propose two useful techniques to enhance performance. For transcription, we initialize the Wardaman token from Sundanese, a language that shares similar phonemes with Wardaman, to accelerate fine-tuning of the transcription model. For translation, we compile a Wardaman-English dictionary from expert annotations, and provide this domain-specific knowledge to a large language model (LLM) to reason and decide the final output. We empirically demonstrate that this two-stage design works better than data-hungry unified approaches in extremely low data settings. Using a mere 6 hours of annotated data, WARDEN outperforms larger open-source and proprietary models and establishes a strong baseline. Data and code are available.

</details>

### [61] [Multiconic Optimization for Symmetric Cones and Hyperbolic Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12658)
*Marianna E. -Nagy,Yurii Nesterov,Petra Renáta Rigó*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于抛物线目标空间方法和超共轭概念的新内点算法，用于求解多锥优化问题，并证明其复杂度与同维度线性规划的最佳已知复杂度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多锥优化问题（可行锥由多个小维锥的直积构成）的大规模求解需求，现有方法在处理这类结构时存在局限性，需要更可控的原始-对偶变量耦合机制。

Method: 引入"超共轭"（hyperbolic coupling）概念，构建基于抛物线目标空间（parabolic target space approach）的内点算法框架，实现原始-对偶变量对的相互依赖耦合，增强行为可控性。

Result: 算法整体复杂度与同维度线性规划问题的最佳已知复杂度相当。

Conclusion: 该算法为大规模多锥优化问题提供了一种高效的求解途径，其理论复杂度达到最优水平。

Abstract: We develop a new interior-point algorithm for solving multiconic optimization problems using the parabolic target space approach. The feasible cone in these problems is composed as a direct product of many small-dimensional cones. Our approach is based on a new concept, called the hyperbolic coupling. This provides a new framework that has an advantage of interdependent pairs of primal-dual variables. In this way, their behaviour is much more controllable. We justify all main steps in the complexity analysis of the algorithm and prove that the overall complexity of solving this type of large-scale nonlinear problems by our algorithm is comparable with the best known complexity for solving linear programming problems of the same dimension.

</details>


### [62] [Newton methods beyond Hessian Lipschitz continuity: A nonlinear preconditioning approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12666)
*Alexander Bodard,Panagiotis Patrinos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类非线性预条件牛顿方法，通过将牛顿根求解方案应用于变换后的最优性映射，在预条件Hessian的Lipschitz连续性假设下（比经典Hessian Lipschitz连续性更宽松）建立局部超线性/二次收敛保证，并设计了全局化策略和自适应正则化变体，达到O(ε^{-3/2})的迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典牛顿型方法通常要求Hessian满足Lipschitz连续性，但该假设对于具有高阶或多项式增长的目标函数可能不成立。作者希望引入一种更宽松的广义光滑性模型，在预条件Hessian的Lipschitz连续性下分析牛顿方法的收敛性，从而处理更广泛的目标函数类别。

Method: 将一阶方法的非线性预条件思想推广到二阶设置，对变换后的最优性映射应用牛顿根求解方案，提出非正则化与正则化两种变体。非正则化方法设计了全局化策略以处理预条件牛顿方向未必是下降方向的问题；正则化变体针对各向同性预条件子，并开发了自适应版本以消除对光滑性常数的依赖并允许子问题非精确求解。

Result: 在预条件Hessian Lipschitz连续性下建立了局部超线性和二次收敛保证；正则化变体达到O(ε^{-3/2})的迭代复杂度；自适应版本在保持相同复杂度阶次的同时，无需先验知道光滑性常数且允许非精确子问题求解。

Conclusion: 非线性预条件牛顿方法通过放松经典Hessian Lipschitz假设，扩展了牛顿型方法的适用范围，为处理更一般的光滑性模型提供了理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: Newton-type methods are typically analyzed under Lipschitz continuity of the Hessian, an assumption that can fail for objectives with higher-order or polynomial growth. We introduce a class of nonlinearly preconditioned Newton methods that apply Newton's root-finding scheme to a transformed optimality mapping, thereby extending recent nonlinear preconditioning ideas from first-order methods to the second-order setting. The resulting methods are naturally analyzed under Lipschitz continuity of a preconditioned Hessian, a condition that significantly relaxes the classical Hessian Lipschitz continuity assumption. Under this generalized smoothness model, we establish local superlinear and quadratic convergence guarantees, and develop a globalization strategy for the nonregularized method despite the fact that the preconditioned Newton direction need not be a descent direction. We further propose a regularized variant for isotropic preconditioners, and show that it attains an $O(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$ iteration complexity. An adaptive version removes the need to know the smoothness constant and allows inexact subproblem solutions while preserving the same complexity order.

</details>


### [63] [Asymptotic KKT Conditions for Continuous-Time Nonlinear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12751)
*Moisés R. C. do Monte,Rodrigo B. Moreira,Valeriano A. de Oliveira*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对含等式与不等式约束的连续时间非线性规划问题，提出了序贯形式的必要最优性条件（渐近满足KKT条件），并在凸性假设下证明了该条件的充分性。基于此，设计了一种增广拉格朗日型数值求解方法，给出了收敛性分析，并通过文献中的实例验证了方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间非线性规划问题缺乏实用的终止判据，传统最优性条件难以直接指导数值方法的收敛判断。本文旨在建立序贯最优性条件，为数值求解方法提供理论终止依据。

Method: 1) 提出序贯KKT条件：证明存在收敛到最优解的解序列，使其渐近满足KKT条件；2) 在凸性假设下证明该条件的充分性；3) 设计增广拉格朗日型数值算法；4) 给出算法的可行性和最优性收敛分析。

Result: 序贯最优性条件为连续时间规划问题提供了可操作的终止准则；所提增广拉格朗日方法在理论上有收敛保证，并在文献实例中表现良好。

Conclusion: 序贯KKT条件有效弥补了连续时间非线性规划在数值求解终止判据方面的空白，增广拉格朗日方法具有实际可行性，为后续算法设计提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper addresses the class of continuous-time nonlinear programming problems with equality and inequality constraints. The paper presents necessary optimality conditions of the sequential form. To be more precise, a sequence of solutions converging to the optimal solution is demonstrated to exist, and such that Karush-Kuhn-Tucker-type conditions are satisfied asymptotically. It is shown that these sequential Karush-Kuhn-Tucker-type conditions also become sufficient for optimality under convexity assumptions. Sequential optimality conditions are a valuable tool for determining when to terminate a numerical method of solution. In this regard, an augmented Lagrangian-type method is proposed for numerically solving continuous-time programming problems. A convergence analysis concerning viability and optimality is presented. The performance of the method is evaluated by applying it to solve instances of continuous-time problems found in the literature.

</details>


### [64] [Uncovering Symmetry Transfer in Large Language Models via Layer-Peeled Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12756)
*Zhehang Du,Hangfeng He,Weijie Su*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM预训练中交叉熵损失函数是否能在模型权重和上下文嵌入中诱导出几何结构。通过分析约束层剥离优化问题，证明目标next-token分布中的对称性（循环移位对称性和置换对称性）会以群论意义精确传递到全局最优解中，使得最优logit矩阵、输出投影矩阵和上下文嵌入的Gram矩阵呈现循环矩阵或单形等角紧框架等几何结构。实验表明开源LLM自然展现出与理论预测一致的对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM预训练中最小化交叉熵损失这一优化策略是否能在模型权重和上下文嵌入中诱导出可解释的几何结构，从而理解LLM内部表征的底层组织原理。

Method: 构建约束层剥离优化程序作为LLM的数学可处理替代模型，将输出投影矩阵和最后一层上下文嵌入作为优化变量。通过非凸优化分析，利用群论工具将目标token分布的对称性传递到全局最优解。对循环对称性：将非凸因子化问题约化为logit层凸优化；对置换对称性：基于对称性的下界加最优因子化刻画。

Result: (1) 当目标token具有循环移位对称性时，最优logit矩阵为循环矩阵，输出投影和上下文嵌入的Gram矩阵也是循环几何结构；(2) 当目标分布具有交换不变性或二传递群作用不变性时，最优输出投影矩阵形成单形等角紧框架，logit矩阵和上下文嵌入继承置换对称性；(3) 开源LLM中自然观察到与理论一致的对称性。

Conclusion: LLM预训练即使没有显式正则化，交叉熵损失本身就能在最优解中诱导出与数据对称性一致的精确几何结构，揭示了大模型表征中的内在对称性原理。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are pretrained by minimizing the cross-entropy loss for next-token prediction. In this paper, we study whether this optimization strategy can induce geometric structure in the learned model weights and context embeddings. We approach this problem by analyzing a constrained layer-peeled optimization program, which serves as a mathematically tractable surrogate for LLMs by treating the output projection matrix and last-layer context embeddings as optimization variables. Our analysis of this nonconvex optimization program demonstrates that symmetries in the target next-token distributions are transferred to the global minimizers of the layer-peeled model in a precise group-theoretic sense. Specifically, we prove that when the target tokens exhibit a cyclic-shift symmetry (such as the seven days of the week or the twelve months of the year), the optimal logit matrix is exactly circulant, and the Gram matrices of both the output projections and the context embeddings form circulant geometries as well. Next, for exchangeable target distributions invariant under the symmetric group and, more generally, under two-transitive group actions, we show that the global optimal output projection matrix forms a simplex equiangular tight frame, while the optimal logit matrix and context embeddings inherit the permutation symmetries present in the input data. A key technical step is to reduce the constrained nonconvex factorized problem to an explicit logit-level convex characterization for cyclic symmetry and to a symmetry-based lower bound for permutation symmetry, together with a sharp characterization of the optimal factorization. Finally, we empirically demonstrate that open-source LLMs naturally exhibit symmetries consistent with our theoretical predictions, despite being trained without any explicit regularization promoting such geometric structure.

</details>


### [65] [Indefinite Stochastic LQ Optimal Control for Jump-Diffusion Systems with Random Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12775)
*Xinyu Ma,Qingxin Meng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有随机系数的跳扩散系统的不定随机线性二次（LQ）最优控制问题。通过构造代数逆流、提取值函数的半鞅核，推导出广义随机Riccati方程（含跳，SREJ）。在一致凸性条件下，证明了开环最优控制的存在唯一性，并给出了基于SREJ的精确闭环反馈表示。该方法无需松弛技术或额外可逆性假设，且允许控制进入跳部分（F≠0）。作为应用，分析了超额收益为零的跳扩散风险资产的金融投资组合问题，将一致凸性条件转化为风险厌恶系数、波动率、跳幅度和无风险利率之间的显式不等式，刻画了最优策略存在的参数区域。这些结果将经典不定LQ理论推广到随机系数的跳扩散系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典不定线性二次最优控制理论从连续扩散系统推广至更一般的跳扩散系统，并处理随机系数情形。现有方法（如补偿器方法）依赖松弛技术或可逆性假设，且难以处理控制进入跳部分（F≠0）的一般情况，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从零控制基础系统构造代数逆流，提取值函数的半鞅核，证明其满足广义随机Riccati方程（含跳，SREJ）。基于一致凸性条件建立开环最优控制的存在唯一性，并通过SREJ给出精确闭环反馈表示。无需补偿器方法中的松弛技巧，也无需对最优状态过程施加额外可逆性假设。

Result: 在一致凸性条件下，证明了开环最优控制对任意初始对存在且唯一；关联矩阵𝒩(t)一致正定，从而通过SREJ获得最优控制的精确闭环反馈表示。在金融投资组合应用中，一致凸性条件简化为风险厌恶系数、波动率、跳幅度和无风险利率之间的显式不等式，清晰刻画了最优策略存在的参数区域。

Conclusion: 本文成功将经典不定LQ理论推广至随机系数的跳扩散系统，提出了一种不依赖松弛技术或额外可逆性假设的新方法，且能处理控制进入跳部分的一般情形。理论成果在金融投资组合问题中得到了具体应用验证。

Abstract: This paper studies indefinite stochastic linear-quadratic (LQ) optimal control for jump-diffusion systems with random coefficients. We construct an algebraic inverse flow from the zero-control base system, extract the semimartingale kernel of the value function, and prove that it satisfies a generalized stochastic Riccati equation with jumps (SREJ). Under a uniform convexity condition, we establish the existence and uniqueness of open-loop optimal controls for any initial pair and show that the associated matrix $\mathscr{N}(t)$ is uniformly positive definite, yielding an exact closed-loop feedback representation of the optimal control via the SREJ. A distinguishing feature of our approach is that it requires neither relaxation techniques (as in the compensator method) nor additional invertibility assumptions on the optimal state process, and it accommodates the general case where the control enters the jump part ($F \neq 0$). As an application, we analyze a financial portfolio problem with a jump-diffusion risky asset whose excess return is zero, where the investor minimizes a cost functional with a negative terminal wealth weight. The uniform convexity condition reduces to an explicit inequality among the risk aversion coefficient, volatility, jump magnitude, and risk-free rate, thereby delineating the parametric region in which an optimal strategy exists. These results extend classical indefinite LQ theory to jump-diffusion systems with random coefficients.

</details>


### [66] [Accuracy and Relationships of Quadratic Models in Derivative-free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12819)
*Yiwen Chen,Warren Hare,Lindon Roberts*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了模型信赖域无导数优化中的三种二次模型（最小范数、最小Frobenius范数和二次广义单纯形导数模型），建立了完全线性误差界，推导了沿采样方向的方向性完全二次精度，并揭示了模型间的结构关系与等价条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三种二次模型在无导数优化中被广泛使用，但其逼近精度及相互关系尚未被系统研究，现有MN模型分析需要均匀有界模型Hessian假设，QS模型则完全缺乏相关理论结果。

Method: 通过建立完全线性误差界（去除了MN模型中均匀有界Hessian的假设，并首次导出QS模型的同类结果），进一步利用方向误差界分析Hessian逼近精度，证明在采样集的温和条件下所有三种模型沿样本方向达到完全二次精度。

Result: 去除了MN模型分析中均匀有界Hessian的强假设；首次为QS模型建立了完全线性误差界；揭示了方向性完全二次精度这一现有理论未捕捉到的性质；明确了三种模型在何种条件下等价。

Conclusion: 三种二次模型具有更加清晰和统一的逼近理论框架，其方向性二次精度与结构等价关系为实际应用中的模型选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study three quadratic models in model-based derivative-free optimization: the minimum norm (MN), minimum Frobenius norm (MFN), and quadratic generalized simplex derivative (QS) models. Despite their widespread use, their approximation accuracy and relationships have not been systematically explored. We establish fully linear error bounds for all three models, removing the uniformly bounded model Hessian assumption required in existing MN analyses and deriving the first such results for the QS model. We further analyze Hessian approximation accuracy via directional error bounds, showing that all three models achieve fully quadratic accuracy along sample directions under a mild condition on the sample set. This reveals a form of directional fully quadratic accuracy not captured by existing theory. Finally, we characterize the relationships among these models, identifying conditions under which they coincide and clarifying their structural connections.

</details>

Abstract: This work is about ME, the Method of Ellipcenters. ME was recently introduced by these very authors as a first order accelerated scheme for unconstrained minimization. Its iterates are all centers of ellipses carefully designed to somehow capture ill-conditioning of the underlying optimization problem. In the first article on ME, we were able to prove that it converges with linear rate when the objective function is quadratic and strongly convex, while here we derive convergence for any differentiable strongly convex objective. This investigation was inspired by the great performance of ME in quadratic minimization against steepest descent with exact line search, FISTA, Barzilai-Borwein and Conjugate Gradient. The experiments we carry out now, make ME even more attractive from the numerical point of view. On top of that, the theory seems promising for quite more general settings.

### [68] [Adam-SHANG: A Convergent Adam-Type Method for Stochastic Smooth Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12878)

Conclusion: Adam-SHANG在理论保证和实际性能之间取得了更好的平衡，其结构更新在非凸设定下同样有效。

Abstract: We propose Adam-SHANG, a Lyapunov-guided Adam-type method that couples momentum, adaptive preconditioning, and a curvature-aware correction through a more stable lagged-preconditioner update. For stochastic smooth convex optimization, we prove convergence in expectation under an admissible stepsize condition that can always be satisfied by a conservative spectral bound, without imposing global monotonicity on the second-moment sequence. To obtain a less conservative practical rule, we introduce a computable trace-ratio stepsize, motivated by a local coordinatewise alignment condition. The same structural update is also tested beyond the convex setting with simplified parameters. Experiments validate the predicted stochastic decay and show competitive training performance against Adam and AdamW on deep learning tasks.

</details>

Method: 1) 先建立时变折现因子和可能无界随机终端时间下BSDE的适定性与稳定性；2) 通过Elliott-Kalton非预期策略和BSDE递归支付定义上下值函数；3) 采用有限时域逼近、BSDE稳定性估计和粘性解论证证明值函数是确定的且为HJBI方程的粘性解；4) 利用非自治HJBI方程的唯一性（而非概率移位论证）从PDE角度恢复自治情形。

Result: 证明了上下值函数是确定性函数，且是相应非自治HJBI方程的唯一有界粘性解；时间齐次情形作为特例被恢复，且值函数不依赖于初始时间并满足稳态HJBI方程。

Abstract: In this paper, we study an infinite horizon non-autonomous stochastic recursive differential game. To this end, we first establish well-posedness and stability results for BSDEs with a time-dependent discount factor and a possibly unbounded random terminal time. The generator $f$ is allowed to be non-uniformly bounded at the origin, namely, $|f(t,0,0)|\les β_1(t)+β_2,$ $t\in[0,\infty),$ $\dbP\text{-a.s.},$ with $β_1\in L^1(0,\infty)$ and $β_2\ges0$. We then formulate a two-person zero-sum stochastic recursive differential game on the infinite horizon, where the drift, diffusion, generator and discount factor may depend explicitly on time. The lower and upper value functions are defined through Elliott--Kalton nonanticipative strategies and BSDE recursive payoffs. By finite horizon approximation, BSDE stability estimates and viscosity solution arguments, we prove that both the lower and upper value functions are deterministic and are the unique bounded viscosity solutions of their corresponding non-autonomous HJBI equations. Finally, the time-homogeneous case is recovered as a special case. Using the uniqueness of the non-autonomous HJBI equation, rather than a probabilistic shift argument, we show from the PDE viewpoint that the value functions of the autonomous system are independent of the initial time and solve the corresponding stationary HJBI equations in the viscosity sense.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 均场项与随机系数的相互作用使得传统解耦技术无法直接应用，需要发展新的理论框架和数值方法来求解此类Stackelberg博弈问题。

Method: 采用扩展拉格朗日乘子法推导从动者最优响应的仿射算子表示；将领导者问题建模为算子值系数的广义随机LQ控制问题；通过无Riccati的耦合FBSDE系统刻画Stackelberg最优控制；提出深度FBSDE Picard求解器，包含从动者响应学习、响应灵敏度提取、领导者优化以及神经增强拉格朗日均场一致性约束强制执行四个模块。

Result: 数值实验覆盖了收敛性诊断、离散化敏感性、Riccati校准、消融测试、局部均衡验证、Stackelberg-Nash对比以及金融应用案例，结果支持了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩展拉格朗日乘子法与深度FBSDE Picard求解器能够有效求解随机均场线性二次Stackelberg微分博弈，为均场博弈与随机控制交叉领域提供了新的理论工具和数值方法。

Abstract: This paper studies a stochastic mean-field linear-quadratic Stackelberg differential game with random coefficients. The interaction between mean-field terms and random coefficients precludes the direct use of conventional decoupling techniques. We apply an extended Lagrange multiplier method to derive an affine operator representation of the follower's optimal response. The induced leader problem is then formulated as a generalized stochastic LQ control problem with operator-valued coefficients, and the Stackelberg optimal control is characterized through a Riccati-free coupled FBSDE system. We further develop a Deep FBSDE Picard Solver that preserves the Stackelberg order through follower-response learning, response-sensitivity extraction, leader optimization, and neural augmented Lagrangian enforcement of mean-field consistency constraints. Numerical studies covering convergence diagnostics, discretization sensitivity, Riccati calibration, ablation tests, local equilibrium validation, Stackelberg--Nash comparisons, and a financial application support the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [71] [Guaranteed cost structured control in infinite-horizon linear-quadratic cooperative differential games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13103)
*Aniruddha Roy,Pavankumar Tallapragada*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对无限时域线性二次型合作微分博弈，在输出反馈信息结构下提出反馈保证代价结构控制（GCSC）概念。证明了帕累托最优控制（若存在）属于GCSC类，量化了其次优性，并给出了GCSC的验证与综合条件，通过数值算例和微电网跟踪同步案例验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输出反馈信息结构下，即使低维合作微分博弈，计算帕累托最优控制也非常困难，亟需一种可行的替代方案。

Method: 引入反馈保证代价结构控制（GCSC）概念，受次优控制启发，在满足结构约束下保证总加权团队代价低于预设阈值；推导了GCSC与容许权重集的基本性质及单调性，证明帕累托最优控制属于GCSC类，并量化次优性。

Result: 证明了帕累托最优控制与GCSC的关系，给出了GCSC的验证与综合条件，数值算例和微电网案例验证了有效性。

Conclusion: GCSC为输出反馈结构下难以直接计算帕累托最优控制的问题提供了一种可行的次优控制框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider infinite-horizon linear-quadratic cooperative differential games with output feedback information structure. We first demonstrate that, under output feedback information structure, computing Pareto optimal controls can be difficult even for simple low-dimensional differential games. To address this issue, this paper introduces the concept of feedback guaranteed cost structured control (GCSC). The feedback GCSC concept is inspired from suboptimal control. At a feedback GCSC, the total weighted team cost remains below a prescribed threshold while satisfying the structural constraints. We derive fundamental properties of the feedback GCSC and the admissible weight set, including their monotonicity properties. In particular, we show that if Pareto optimal controls exist, they belong to the class of feedback GCSCs. We also quantify the suboptimalty of Pareto optimal controls (if they exist) and the proposed GCSC with respect to output feedback optimal control. Furthermore, we provide the conditions for verification and the synthesis of a feedback GCSC. Finally, we illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach through numerical examples, including a case study on tracking synchronization in a microgrid.

</details>


### [72] [Recasting AI Data Centers as Engines for Carbon Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13114)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心的电力需求及其CO₂排放快速增长，同时这些数据中心持续产生大量低品位废热。如何利用这些废热来抵消AI基础设施的碳足迹、降低直接空气碳捕集的成本，是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 开发了一个热力学集成的DAC-AIDC系统模型，并在美国范围内进行区域解析评估，考虑了AIDC容量、服务器组成、当地气候、电价和电网碳强度等变量。

Result: AIDC废热可显著提高净CO₂去除率并降低捕集成本；在碳密集型区域，集成可使DAC从净正排放转为净负排放；2030年场景下，多个州实现去除比大于1。

Conclusion: 集成AIDC废热与DAC系统可在降低碳捕集成本的同时实现净负碳排放，是缓解AI基础设施气候影响的有效路径。

Abstract: AI data centers (AIDCs) are rapidly increasing electricity demand and associated CO2 emissions, yet they also generate continuous low-grade waste heat. Here, we assess whether this heat can be upgraded by heat pumps to drive direct air capture (DAC) and reduce the climate impact of AI infrastructure. We develop a thermodynamically integrated DAC-AIDC system and conduct a region-resolved assessment across the United States, accounting for AIDC capacity, server composition, local climate, electricity prices, and grid carbon intensity. We find that AIDC waste heat can substantially improve net CO2 removal and lower the levelized cost of capture. In carbon-intensive regions, integration can flip DAC from net-positive to net-negative. Under a 2030 scenario with more GPU-intensive AIDCs and cleaner grids, several states achieve removal ratios above 1, indicating that integrated systems can offset their own operational emissions and deliver additional carbon removal.

</details>


### [73] [Indefinite Stochastic Linear-Quadratic Optimal Control Problems with Random Coefficients and Poisson Jumps: Closed-Loop Representation of Open-Loop Optimal Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13204)
*Kai Ding,Jiaqiang Wen,Jie Xiong,Xin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了含随机系数和泊松跳的有限时域随机线性二次最优控制问题，其中加权矩阵可以是随机且不定的。在成本泛函满足一致凸性条件下，证明了带跳的随机Riccati方程存在唯一强正则解，进而开环最优控制具有闭环表示。证明不依赖于P=YX^{-1}的全局表示或状态方程中跳跃乘子I_n+E的非奇异条件，而是通过随机价值流构造P，并利用小区间局部化方法推导Riccati解的强正则性。此外，给出了一致凸性的充分条件，并举例说明了不定终端和控制加权矩阵以及SRE中非零跳鞅分量。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机线性二次最优控制研究大多假设加权矩阵半正定或跳跃乘子非奇异，限制了模型在金融等实际场景中的应用。本文致力于放宽这些假设，研究加权矩阵可能为不定矩阵、跳跃乘子可能奇异的一般情形。

Method: (1) 引入成本泛函的一致凸性条件替代传统的半正定加权假设；(2) 通过随机价值流构造Riccati解P，而非依赖P=YX^{-1}的全局表示；(3) 采用小区间局部化方法推导解的强正则性，避免对跳跃乘子I_n+E的非奇异假设。

Result: (1) 在一致凸性条件下，带跳的随机Riccati方程存在唯一强正则解；(2) 开环最优控制可表示为闭环形式；(3) 给出一致凸性的可验证充分条件；(4) 通过数值例子验证了不定加权矩阵和非零跳鞅分量的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一般化条件下含跳随机线性二次最优控制问题的完整理论框架，所提方法避开了传统路径依赖，为更广泛的实际应用（如带跳的金融模型）提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper studies finite-horizon stochastic linear-quadratic optimal control problems with random coefficients and Poisson jumps, where the weighting matrices may be random and indefinite. Under a uniform convexity condition on the cost functional, we prove that the associated stochastic Riccati equation (SRE) with jumps admits a unique strongly regular solution. As a consequence, the open-loop optimal control admits a closed-loop representation. The proof does not rely on a global representation of the form $P=\mathbf Y\mathbf X^{-1}$ or on any nonsingularity condition on the jump multiplier $I_n+E$ in the state equation. Instead, we construct $P$ from the stochastic value flow, and derive the strong regularity of the Riccati solution by a small-interval localization method. In addition, sufficient conditions are obtained for uniform convexity, and examples are presented to illustrate indefinite terminal and control weighting matrices and a nonzero jump martingale component in the SRE.

</details>

*Boyang Zhang,Zhiguo Wang,Ya-Feng Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于近似的生成建模（PGM）框架，通过建立高斯卷积与Moreau-Yosida正则化之间的理论等价性，引入具有闭式表达的Moreau分数，避免了扩散模型中难以处理的似然分数计算，并在逆问题上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数的扩散模型在逆问题中面临时间依赖似然分数解析难处理的根本瓶颈，现有方法需要显式似然评估，限制了其在逆问题中的应用性能。

Method: 1) 建立扩散过程中高斯卷积与非光滑优化中Moreau-Yosida正则化之间的理论等价性；2) 提出Moreau分数，通过近端算子获得闭式表达；3) 引入Moreau分数匹配学习仅依赖于先验分布样本的近端算子。

Result: PGM消除了基于分数的扩散模型固有的早期停止偏差，实现了非渐近收敛，并在重建质量和采样时间上显著优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: PGM框架通过规避显式似然评估，从理论上和实验上解决了扩散模型在逆问题中的核心瓶颈，提供了一种更高效、更精确的生成建模替代方案。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models demonstrate superior performance in generative tasks but encounter fundamental bottlenecks in inverse problems due to the analytical intractability of the time-dependent likelihood score. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel proximal-based generative modeling (PGM) framework that rigorously circumvents explicit likelihood evaluation. Our framework is built upon a theoretical equivalence between Gaussian convolution in diffusion processes and Moreau-Yosida regularization in nonsmooth optimization. This enables a new sampling mechanism driven by the proposed Moreau score, which admits a closed-form expression via proximal operators. Moreover, we introduce Moreau score matching to learn the proximal operators that rely solely on samples drawn from the prior distribution. Theoretically, PGM eliminates the early-stopping bias inherent in the score-based diffusion model and achieves non-asymptotic convergence. Experiments demonstrate that PGM significantly surpasses state-of-the-art methods in reconstruction quality and sampling time.

</details>


### [75] [Affiliated operators for classical and quantum control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13774)
*Dimitrios Giannakis,Gage Hoefer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用冯·诺依曼代数理论，提出了一种在无限维希尔伯特空间上研究双线性系统可控性的框架。在漂移项和控制项均与有限型冯·诺依曼代数关联的假设下，证明了时间最优控制的存在性，并展示了如何通过动力李代数检验系统的近似可控性。通过Koopman算子形式应用于经典动力学系统，并举例说明了关联关系在经典与量子控制系统中的自然出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无限维双线性系统的可控性理论缺乏统一的框架，特别是在处理无界算子以及经典-量子系统统一描述方面存在困难。作者希望借助冯·诺依曼代数的成熟数学工具来建立更通用的理论框架。

Method: 采用冯·诺依曼代数（有限型）作为核心数学框架，将系统算子视为与该代数关联的（可能无界的）算子；利用动力李代数分析近似可控性；通过Koopman算子形式桥接经典动力学系统。

Result: 在控制项满足范数有界条件时，证明了时间最优控制的存在性；在算子可能无界的一般情形下，证明了动力李代数仍可良好定义并用于检验近似可控性。

Conclusion: 该框架为无限维双线性可控性提供了统一的理论工具，既涵盖经典系统（通过Koopman算子）也涵盖量子系统，且关联关系在两类系统中均自然出现，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Using techniques from the theory of von Neumann algebras, we propose a framework for addressing questions of controllability of bilinear systems on infinite dimensional Hilbert spaces. In the setup, we assume only that the drift and control terms arising in a bilinear control system are affiliated with a von Neumann algebra of finite type acting on the same Hilbert space. When the control terms satisfy basic norm bound conditions, we prove existence of time-optimal controls. In the more general setting where all operators may be unbounded, we show how the dynamical Lie algebra for the system is still well-defined and may be used to check approximate controllability of the system in question. We discuss how this approach can be applied to classical dynamical systems through the Koopman operator formalism, and investigate potential candidates for the von Neumann algebra which may guide the choice of controls. We illustrate how an affiliation relation naturally arises in both classical and quantum control systems with a few examples.

</details>

### [76] [Learning When to Act: Communication-Efficient Reinforcement Learning via Run-Time Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12561)
*Adam Haroon,Erick J. Rodríguez-Seda,Cody Fleming,Tristan Schuler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全强化学习框架，通过李雅普诺夫安全屏蔽层让单一策略同时学习控制输入和通信高效的时间决策，在多平台上实现了比基线方法更高的平均采样间隔，并证明了自适应时序而非低平均频率使稀疏控制变得安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全强化学习关注智能体"做什么"，本文转而关注"何时需要行动"，旨在通过自适应时序决策实现通信高效的稳定控制。

Method: 基于点态李雅普诺夫安全屏蔽层（RTA）的单一策略，联合学习控制输入和通信时序决策；使用CARE推导的LQR备份和一步前向李雅普诺夫预测提供严格安全保证；提出偏好条件扩展以获取完整权衡前沿。

Result: 在倒立摆、小车-杆和平面四旋翼上，MSI分别比李雅普诺夫触发基线提高1.91×、1.45×和3.51×；固定LQR在相同平均频率下不稳定；移除RTA后MSI降低1.27-1.84×且状态范数恶化；偏好扩展仅需2/11训练计算量。

Conclusion: 自适应时序决策是实现安全稀疏控制的关键，RTA安全屏蔽层不可或缺；该方法算法无关，可扩展到更高维系统（如12状态3D四旋翼），并对模型误差具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) typically asks $\textit{what}$ an agent should do. We ask $\textit{when}$ it needs to act, and show that a single policy can jointly learn control inputs and communication-efficient timing decisions under a pointwise Lyapunov safety shield. We focus on stabilization around a known equilibrium, where CARE-based LQR backups, Lyapunov certificates, and classical Lyapunov-STC are well defined, enabling clean comparison against analytical baselines. A run-time assurance (RTA) layer overrides the policy via a one-step-ahead Lyapunov prediction and a precomputed LQR backup, providing a strictly stronger guarantee than constrained MDP methods that enforce safety only in expectation. On an inverted pendulum, cart--pole, and planar quadrotor, the learned policy achieves $1.91\times$, $1.45\times$, and $3.51\times$ higher mean inter-sample interval (MSI) than a Lyapunov-triggered baseline; a fixed LQR controller at the same average rate is unstable on all three plants, showing that adaptive timing, not a lower average rate, makes sparsity safe. A CARE-derived Lyapunov reward transfers across environments without redesign, with a single weight $w_c$ controlling the stability--communication tradeoff; ablations confirm the RTA shield is essential, with its removal reducing MSI by $1.27$--$1.84\times$ and degrading state norms. A preference-conditioned extension recovers the full tradeoff frontier from one model at $\tfrac{2}{11}$ of training compute, and SAC experiments show the results are algorithm-agnostic across discrete and continuous domains. A 12-state 3D quadrotor case study extends the framework to higher-dimensional systems where classical STC is intractable, and robustness to $\pm30\%$ mass variation and disturbances shows graceful degradation, with the RTA absorbing what the learned policy cannot.

</details>


### [77] [Towards Robust Federated Multimodal Graph Learning under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12584)
