Frame-level Grad-CAM localized salient evidence to the aortic root and leaflet plane, while globally aggregated SHAP values quantified each video backbone's contribution to the stacked prediction, enabling transparent, case-level auditability. These findings indicate that PLAX-based video ensembles can support reliable BAV/TAV classification from routine echocardiographic cine loops and may facilitate earlier detection in non-specialist or resource-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [187] [Path-independent Flow Matching for Multi-parameter Generative Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13487)
*Francisco Téllez,AmirHossein Zamani,Philippe Martin,Shuang Ni,Guy Wolf,Eugene Belilovsky,Sina Sanjari,Yanlei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出路径无关流匹配（PiFM），将标准流匹配推广到多参数场景，确保变换结果仅依赖于初始和目标分布而不依赖具体路径，并证明其在合适假设下能近似Wasserstein重心。通过可计算的仿真无关目标函数，PiFM在合成数据和真实数据上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配（Flow Matching）的单参数形式无法处理多参数变化场景，且变换结果依赖于路径，缺乏路径无关性。路径无关性对于确保变换仅取决于初始分布和目标分布、不依赖具体路径至关重要，因此需要一种新的框架来同时处理多参数变化并保证路径无关性。

Method: 提出路径无关流匹配（PiFM），将流匹配推广到高维参数域，通过强制结构条件确保组合变换的一致性。设计了可计算的、仿真无关的目标函数，用于回归多参数条件概率路径。在理论上证明了PiFM在适当假设下可近似Wasserstein重心，从而连接了分布插值的概念。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上，PiFM在插值路径无关轨迹和生成期望的分布外样本方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PiFM成功地将流匹配扩展到多参数场景，实现了路径无关的分布变换，同时与Wasserstein重心建立了理论联系。该框架具有实用可训练性，并在实验中表现优异。

Abstract: Flow Matching is a powerful framework for learning transport maps between probability distributions. Yet its standard single-parameter formulation is not designed to capture multi-parameter variations where the resulting transport should be path-independent. Path independence is crucial because it ensures that transformations depend only on the initial and target distributions, not on the specific path. In this work, we introduce Path-independent Flow Matching (PiFM), a method for learning vector fields whose induced flows yield path-independent transport between distributions. We show that PiFM generalizes Flow Matching to higher-dimensional parameter domains while enforcing structural conditions that ensure consistency of composed transformations. In addition, we show that, under suitable assumptions, PiFM approximates the Wasserstein barycenter, linking the framework to a notion of distributional interpolation. To enable practical training, we propose a tractable, simulation-free objective that regresses onto multi-parameter conditional probability paths. We showcase empirically that PiFM outperforms other approaches on both synthetic and real world data in interpolating path-independent trajectories and generating desired out of distribution samples.

</details>


### [188] [High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13768)
*Or Ordentlich,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于权重量化的矩阵乘法问题，在已知协方差矩阵Σ_X的情况下，通过水填充（waterfilling）方法改进GPTQ算法，提出了接近信息论极限的WaterSIC方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）的权重量化中，传统方法（如GPTQ）在坐标间均匀分配量化比特数，未能利用协方差信息实现最优分配；本文旨在通过水填充方法优化比特分配，提升量化性能。

Method: 将权重量化问题建模为加权均方误差（WMSE）信源编码问题，利用经典的反向水填充（reverse waterfilling）解来指导比特分配；分析WaterSIC方案（仅使用标量INT量化器），证明其高率性能与基无关（由det(Σ_X)决定），且与信息论失真极限仅差一个乘法因子2πe/12（约0.25 bit/entry）。

Result: WaterSIC的高率性能与基无关，不受随机旋转影响；GPTQ在随机旋转下（以Llama-3-8B实际Σ_X测试）性能与WaterSIC相差约0.1 bit（依层类型而异），表明随机旋转后的GPTQ在高率区间也接近最优。

Conclusion: 水填充方法可有效改进LLM权重量化；WaterSIC方案在理论上接近信息论极限；GPTQ配合随机旋转在实际中亦接近最优性能。

Abstract: This is the second part of the work investigating quantized matrix multiplication (MatMul). In part I we considered the case of calibration-free quantization, whereas here we discuss the setting where covariance matrix $Σ_X$ of the columns of the second factor is available. This setting arises in the ubiquitous task of weight-only post-training quantization of LLMs.
  Weight-only quantization is related to the problem of weighted mean squared error (WMSE) source coding, whose classical (reverse) waterfilling solution dictates how one should distribute rate between coordinates of the vector. We show how waterfilling can be used to improve practical LLM quantization algorithms (GPTQ), which at present allocate rate equally. A recent scheme (known as ``WaterSIC'') that only uses scalar INT quantizers is analyzed and its high-rate performance is shown to be (a) basis free (i.e., characterized by the determinant of $Σ_X$ and, thus, unlike existing schemes, is immune to applying random rotations); and (b) within a multiplicative factor of $\frac{2πe}{12}$ (or 0.25 bit/entry) of the information-theoretic distortion limit. GPTQ's performance, in turn, is affected by the choice of basis, but for a random rotation and actual $Σ_X$ from Llama-3-8B we find it to be within 0.1 bit (depending on the layer type) of WaterSIC, suggesting that GPTQ with random rotation is also near optimal, at least in the high-rate regime.

</details>


### [189] [MinT: Managed Infrastructure for Training and Serving Millions of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13779)
*Mind Lab,:,Song Cao,Vic Cao,Andrew Chen,Kaijie Chen,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Kaixuan Fan,Hera Feng,Huan Feng,Arthur Fu,Jun Gao,Hongquan Gu,Aaron Guan,Nolan Ho,Mutian Hong,Hailee Hou,Peixuan Hua,Charles Huang,Miles Jiang,Nora Jiang,Yuyi Jiang,Qiuyu Jin,Fancy Kong,Andrew Lei,Kyrie Lei,Alexy Li,Lucian Li,Ray Li,Theo Li,Zhihui Li,Jiayi Lin,Kairus Liu,Kieran Liu,Logan Liu,Xiang Liu,Irvine Lu,Maeve Luo,Runze Lv,Pony Ma,Verity Niu,Anson Qiu,Vincent Wang,Rio Yang,Maxwell Yao,Carrie Ye,Regis Ye,Wenlin Ye,Josh Ying,Danney Zeng,Yuhan Zhan,Anya Zhang,Di Zhang,Ruijia Zhang,Sueky Zhang,Ya Zhang,Wei Zhao,Ada Zhou,Changhai Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Xinyue Zhu,Murphy Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MinT是一个管理LoRA后训练和在线服务的基础设施系统，支持在共享的基础模型上高效处理百万级LoRA策略目录，通过扩展上、下、外三个维度实现规模化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA策略管理需要将每个策略物化为完整检查点，导致存储和计算开销巨大。作者希望构建一个系统，让多个训练策略共享少量昂贵的基座模型部署，避免重复加载基础模型。

Method: MinT通过三个维度扩展：(1) Scale Up：将LoRA RL扩展到前沿规模的密集和MoE架构；(2) Scale Down：仅导出LoRA适配器（可小于基座模型1%），实现适配器交接加速；(3) Scale Out：分离策略寻址与计算工作集，支持百万级可寻址目录和千级活跃适配器波次。

Result: 适配器交接在4B密集模型上加速18.3倍，30B MoE上加速2.85倍；多策略GRPO缩短1.77倍和1.45倍墙钟时间而不增加峰值内存；打包MoE LoRA张量提升引擎加载速度8.5-8.7倍。

Conclusion: MinT成功在1T级共享基础模型上管理百万级LoRA策略目录，同时高效训练和服务选定的适配器版本，验证了大规模LoRA策略管理的可行性。

Abstract: We present MindLab Toolkit (MinT), a managed infrastructure system for Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) post-training and online serving. MinT targets a setting where many trained policies are produced over a small number of expensive base-model deployments. Instead of materializing each policy as a merged full checkpoint, MinT keeps the base model resident and moves exported LoRA adapter revisions through rollout, update, export, evaluation, serving, and rollback, hiding distributed training, serving, scheduling, and data movement behind a service interface. MinT scales this path along three axes. Scale Up extends LoRA RL to frontier-scale dense and MoE architectures, including MLA and DSA attention paths, with training and serving validated beyond 1T total parameters. Scale Down moves only the exported LoRA adapter, which can be under 1% of base-model size in rank-1 settings; adapter-only handoff reduces the measured step by 18.3x on a 4B dense model and 2.85x on a 30B MoE, while concurrent multi-policy GRPO shortens wall time by 1.77x and 1.45x without raising peak memory. Scale Out separates durable policy addressability from CPU/GPU working sets: a tensor-parallel deployment supports 10^6-scale addressable catalogs (measured single-engine sweeps through 100K) and thousand-adapter active waves at cluster scale, with cold loading treated as scheduled service work and packed MoE LoRA tensors improving live engine loading by 8.5-8.7x. MinT thus manages million-scale LoRA policy catalogs while training and serving selected adapter revisions over shared 1T-class base models.

</details>


### [190] [ENSEMBITS: an alphabet of protein conformational ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13789)
*Kaiwen Shi,Carlos Oliver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ensembits，首个蛋白质构象系综分词器，通过残差VQ-VAE和帧蒸馏目标在分子动力学语料上训练，能够从动态蛋白质结构中学习离散词汇，在RMSF预测、残基运动幅度分析、功能预测和零样本突变效应预测等任务上超越现有静态分词器，并可从单张预测结构中推断动态信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构分词器仅捕捉静态结构的局部几何特征，忽略了蛋白质系综所揭示的相关运动与替代构象状态，缺乏对蛋白质动态信息的离散化编码能力。

Method: 采用残差VQ-VAE（矢量量化变分自编码器）架构，结合帧蒸馏目标（frame distillation objective），在大规模分子动力学语料上训练。方法核心包括：跨构象的几何描述子提取、变尺寸系综的置换不变编码、以及动力学数据稀疏性克服策略。

Result: 在RMSF预测任务上超越所有相关方法；在基于token的ANOVA检验中，对残基运动幅度预测为最强的独立结构分词器；在EC、GO、结合位点/亲和力预测及零样本突变效应预测上匹配或超越静态分词器，且使用的预训练数据远少于后者。

Conclusion: Ensembits为蛋白质语言建模和设计提供了首个将动力学纳入离散词汇的工具，且蒸馏目标使其能从单张预测结构中预测动态token，缓解了动力学数据稀疏性问题，推动领域从静态结构预测迈向系综生成。

Abstract: Protein structure tokenizers (PSTs) are workhorses in protein language modeling, function prediction, and evolutionary analysis. However, existing PSTs only capture local geometry of static structures, and miss the correlated motions and alternative conformational states revealed by protein ensembles. Here we introduce Ensembits, the first tokenizer of protein conformational ensembles. Ensembits address challenges inherent to tokenizing dynamics: deriving informative geometric descriptors across conformations, permutation-invariance encoding of variable-size ensembles, and conquering sparsity in dynamics data. Trained with a Residual VQ-VAE using a frame distillation objective on a large molecular dynamics corpus, Ensembits outperforms all related methods on RMSF prediction, and is the strongest standalone structural tokenizer on an token-conditioned ANOVA test on per-residue motion amplitude. Ensembits further matches or exceeds static tokenizers on EC, GO, binding site/affinity prediction, and zero-shot mutation-effect prediction despite using far less pretraining data. Notably, the distillation objective enables Ensembits to predict dynamics token from one single predicted structure, which alleviates dynamics data sparsity. As the field moves from static structure prediction toward ensemble generation, Ensembits offer the discrete vocabulary needed to bring dynamics into protein language modeling and design.

</details>


### [191] [Improving Reproducibility in Evaluation through Multi-Level Annotator Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13801)
*Deepak Pandita,Flip Korn,Chris Welty,Christopher M. Homan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式AI模型（如LLM）评估中的可重复性危机，提出了一种多级自助法来建模标注者行为，并通过分析标注项数量（N）与每项标注次数（K）之间的权衡，研究如何实现统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估面临可重复性危机，人工标注者引入偏差和主观意见，且缺乏足够数据研究标注者池扩大如何改善实验可重复性。标准评估实践通常每项仅用3-5条标注，缺乏持久标注者标识符，无法建模个体方差。

Method: 提出多级自助法（multi-level bootstrapping）来真实建模标注者行为，利用包含大量评分和持久标注者标识符的数据集，分析标注项数量N与每项标注次数K之间的权衡，以达到统计显著性。

Result: 通过多级自助法建模，揭示了在有限标注资源下，N和K之间的权衡关系，以及如何配置两者以达到统计显著性的最优方案。

Conclusion: 要提升AI评估的可重复性和统计显著性，需要超越当前3-5条标注的惯例，合理分配标注项数量和每项标注次数，并利用持久标注者标识符来建模个体方差。

Abstract: As generative AI models such as large language models (LLMs) become more pervasive, ensuring the safety, robustness, and overall trustworthiness of these systems is paramount. However, AI is currently facing a reproducibility crisis driven by unreliable evaluations and unrepeatable experimental results. While human raters are often used to assess models for utility and safety, they introduce divergent biases and subjective opinions into their annotations. Overcoming this variance is exceptionally challenging because very little data exists to study how experimental repeatability actually improves as the annotator pool grows. Standard evaluation practices typically rely on a small number of annotations per item (often 3 to 5) and lack the persistent rater identifiers necessary to model individual variance across items. In this work, we introduce a multi-level bootstrapping approach to realistically model annotator behavior. Leveraging datasets with a large number of ratings and persistent rater identifiers, we analyze the tradeoffs between the number of items ($N$) and the number of responses per item ($K$) required to achieve statistical significance.

</details>


### [192] [Spatiotemporal downscaling and nowcasting of urban land surface temperatures with deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13566)
*Solomiia Kurchaba,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合地球静止轨道卫星（SEVIRI/MSG）和极地轨道卫星（Terra/Aqua MODIS）数据，使用U-Net模型将LST从3 km/15 min降尺度至1 km/15 min，并基于ConvLSTM架构实现15-75分钟的超短期预报，在欧洲大城市测试集上RMSE为1.92°C，预报RMSE为0.57-1.15°C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星LST产品无法同时提供高空间分辨率和高时间分辨率，存在根本性权衡，亟需解决这一矛盾以满足城市气候与生态研究等应用需求。

Method: 两步法：(1) 训练U-Net模型，将SEVIRI/MSG的LST场（3 km, 15 min）映射到与MODIS（1 km, 日4次过境）时空对齐的LST场，实现降尺度；(2) 基于ConvLSTM架构构建预报模型，输入降尺度后的LST场，输出15-75分钟超前预报。

Result: U-Net模型在测试集上RMSE=1.92°C，MBE=0.01°C；ConvLSTM预报模型对15-75分钟超前时间RMSE为0.57-1.15°C，偏差-0.1至0.14°C，优于持久性和气候滚动中位数基准，独立MODIS验证表现稳健。

Conclusion: 提出的高时空分辨率LST预报模型可直接应用于业务化卫星LST监测，有效解决了空间与时间分辨率权衡问题。

Abstract: Land Surface Temperature (LST) is a key variable for various applications, such as urban climate and ecology studies. Yet, existing satellite-derived LST products provide either high spatial or high temporal resolution, resulting in a fundamental trade-off between the two. To address this trade-off, we combine observations from a geostationary and a polar orbiting satellite and provide LST fields at high spatial and high temporal resolution (1 km at 15-min intervals). We demonstrate their application for intraday forecasting of LSTs. To estimate LST fields at high spatiotemporal resolution, a U-Net model is trained to map LST fields from SEVIRI/MSG (3 km and 15 min resolution) to LST fields from Terra/Aqua MODIS (1 km, 4 overpasses per day) that are collocated in space and time. The presented model has been trained on LSTs across large European cities with a population exceeding 1 million inhabitants, and achieves an RMSE = $1.92$°C and near-zero bias MBE = $0.01$°C on the hold-out test set. As a second step, we present an LST nowcasting model based on ConvLSTM architecture, trained across downscaled LST fields with forecast lead times of 15 to 75 minutes. The nowcasting model outperforms a persistence and a Climatological Rolling Median benchmarks, with RMSEs of $0.57$ to $1.15$°C for the considered lead times and biases ranging from $-0.1$ to $0.14$°C. An additional validation conducted against independent MODIS overpasses confirms robust performance. Our LST forecast model at high spatiotemporal resolution is directly applicable to operational satellite-based LST monitoring.

</details>


### [193] [Topology-Preserving Neural Operator Learning via Hodge Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13834)
*Dongzhe Zheng,Tao Zhong,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从函数空间视角研究几何网格上的物理场方程求解算子，揭示Hodge正交性通过将不可学习的拓扑自由度与可学习的几何动力学分离，从根本上解决频谱干扰问题，实现结构保持子空间的加性逼近。基于Hodge理论和算子分裂，推导出原理性的算子级分解，提出混合欧拉-拉格朗日架构及名为Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD)的代数级归纳偏置。该框架使用离散微分形式捕获拓扑主导分量，用正交辅助环境空间表示复杂局部动力学，在几何图上实现了更优的精度、效率和物理不变性保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理几何网格上的物理场方程时，可学习的几何动力学与不可学习的拓扑自由度之间存在频谱干扰，影响求解精度和物理保真度。本文试图从函数空间视角揭示这一干扰的根源，并设计一种能够分离两者、保持结构特性的算子学习方法。

Method: 基于Hodge理论和算子分裂，从算子级别推导出将拓扑自由度与几何动力学正交分解的原理性框架。采用离散微分形式捕获拓扑主导分量，同时构建正交辅助环境空间表示复杂局部动力学，形成混合欧拉-拉格朗日架构，其核心归纳偏置称为Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD)。

Result: 在几何图上实现了更优越的精度和计算效率，同时显著提升了物理不变量的保真度。

Conclusion: Hodge正交性可以有效分离拓扑与几何分量，HSD架构作为结构保持的算子学习方法，在物理场求解任务中兼顾精度、效率与物理一致性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study solution operators of physical field equations on geometric meshes from a function-space perspective. We reveal that Hodge orthogonality fundamentally resolves spectral interference by isolating unlearnable topological degrees of freedom from learnable geometric dynamics, enabling an additive approximation confined to structure-preserving subspaces. Building on Hodge theory and operator splitting, we derive a principled operator-level decomposition. The result is a Hybrid Eulerian-Lagrangian architecture with an algebraic-level inductive bias we call Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD). In our framework, we use discrete differential forms to capture topology-dominated components and an orthogonal auxiliary ambient space to represent complex local dynamics. Our method achieves superior accuracy and efficiency on geometric graphs with enhanced fidelity to physical invariants. Our code is available at https://github.com/ContinuumCoder/Hodge-Spectral-Duality

</details>


### [194] [Three-Stage Learning Unlocks Strong Performance in Simple Models for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13678)
*Zhenan Yu,Guangxin Jiang,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STAIR训练范式，通过三阶段渐进式学习（共享时序映射→逐变量微调→残差学习）解锁简单时序模型的能力，在9个长期预测基准上达到或超越复杂模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期时序预测中，许多强基线依赖频域建模、显式分解、多尺度混合等复杂结构先验，但对简单时序映射如何训练和组织关注不足。作者旨在不引入复杂模块的前提下，通过训练策略提升简单模型的能力。

Method: STAIR包含三个阶段：(1) 共享时序映射，学习所有变量间的共同时序动态；(2) 通道级微调，将共享模型适配到每个变量，捕获变量特异性模式；(3) 残差学习，补充跨变量信息。此外提出Shared-to-Individual Fine-tuning和alpha-RevIN，缓解严格通道独立性和标准RevIN过强归一化先验的问题。

Result: 在9个长期预测基准上，STAIR匹配或超越了近期强基线，同时保持简单的时序骨干（浅层MLP），线性变体也有单独分析。

Conclusion: STAIR提供了一个简洁有效的训练视角：通过合理的训练组织和渐进式能力分解，简单模型可以达到甚至超越复杂架构的性能，无需引入额外的结构模块。

Abstract: Recent studies on long-term time series forecasting have shown that simple linear models and MLP-based predictors can achieve strong performance without increasingly complex architectures. However, many competitive baselines still rely on structural priors such as frequency-domain modeling, explicit decomposition, multi-scale mixing, or sophisticated cross-variable interaction modules, while paying less attention to how simple temporal mappings should be trained and organized. In this paper, we propose STAIR, short for Stagewise Temporal Adaptation via Individualization and Residual Learning, a training paradigm for long-term time series forecasting that aims to unlock the capacity of simple temporal mapping models without introducing complex architectural modules. STAIR decomposes forecasting ability into three progressive stages: it first learns common temporal dynamics across variables through a shared temporal mapping, then adapts the shared model to each variable via channel-wise fine-tuning to capture variable-specific patterns, and finally complements the backbone with cross-variable information through residual learning. We further introduce Shared-to-Individual Fine-tuning and alpha-RevIN to mitigate the limitations of strict channel independence and the overly strong normalization prior induced by standard RevIN. This design gradually increases modeling flexibility while keeping the core temporal predictor as a shallow MLP in the main experiments, with linear variants analyzed separately. Experiments on nine long-term forecasting benchmarks show that STAIR matches or outperforms recent strong baselines while preserving a simple temporal backbone, providing a concise and effective modeling perspective for long-term time series forecasting.

</details>


### [195] [Sampling from Flow Language Models via Marginal-Conditioned Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13681)
*Iskander Azangulov,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对流语言模型（FLMs）提出了一种基于后验预测的自然采样器（MCB），该方法在每个反向步骤中从因子化后验分布采样干净的独热端点，并基于该端点通过奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克桥采样下一个连续状态。实验表明，该方法在无需额外训练的情况下改善了质量-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DDPM风格采样器在FLM中会将后验边际坍缩为单一条件均值端点，但该端点通常不是有效的独热序列，导致采样质量受限。本文认为更自然的采样器应基于后验预测进行采样。

Method: 提出后验预测条件桥（MCB）采样器：1）从FLM token边际定义的因子化后验中采样干净独热端点；2）基于该端点从解析的OU桥中采样下一个连续状态。该方法无需训练，与标准采样使用相同模型评估次数，并支持温度缩放和核截断等解码控制。

Result: 证明在精确后验边际下，端点近似误差等于token位置间的条件多信息；一步桥核保留了所有tokenwise后验预测边际，仅丢失残差交叉位置依赖。通过Girsanov路径空间比较证明，边际条件桥的去噪误差项不大于冻结条件均值桥。实验表明MCB改善了质量-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: MCB是FLM更自然且理论上优的采样方案，为token级解码控制提供了有原则的接口，并在实验中取得了更好的效果。

Abstract: Flow Language Models (FLMs) are a recently introduced class of language models which adapt continuous flow matching for one-hot encoded token sequences. Their denoisers have a special structure absent from generic continuous diffusion models: each block of the denoising mean is a posterior marginal distribution over the clean token at that position. Standard DDPM-style samplers collapse these marginals to a single conditional-mean endpoint and bridge toward this simplex-valued point, which is generally not a valid one-hot sequence. We argue that the natural sampler for an FLM is instead posterior-predictive. At each reverse step, we sample a clean one-hot endpoint from the factorized posterior defined by the FLM token marginals, and then sample the next continuous state from the analytic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck bridge conditioned on that endpoint. The method is training-free, uses the same model evaluations as standard sampling, and gives a principled interface for token-level decoding controls such as temperature scaling and nucleus truncation. We show that, under exact posterior marginals, the endpoint approximation error is exactly the conditional multi-information among token positions. The induced one-step bridge kernel preserves all token-wise posterior-predictive marginals and loses only the residual cross-position dependence. Finally, we prove a Girsanov path-space comparison showing that the marginal-conditioned bridge has a no-larger denoising-error term than the frozen conditional-mean bridge, with strict improvement whenever intermediate coordinate-wise bridge observations reveal additional information about the clean token. Experiments with FLMs show that the sampler improves the quality--diversity tradeoff. Code is available at: github.com/imbirik/mcb.

</details>


### [196] [Scale-Sensitive Shattering: Learnability and Evaluability at Optimal Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13684)
*Shashaank Aiyer,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Han Shao,Tom Waknine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了实值函数类在尺度 γ 上的泛化与可学习性的精确刻画：均匀收敛（尺度 γ）、半空间不可知学习（尺度 γ/2）以及脂肪破碎维数有限（任意 γ' > γ）三者等价。这否定了 Phil Long 关于 2 倍乘法因子不可避免的猜想，改进了 Bartlett & Long (1998) 的上界，并解决了 Anthony & Bartlett (1999)、Alon et al. (1997) 和 Rudelson & Vershynin (2006) 的公开问题。关键技术是直接绑定经验 ℓ∞ 覆盖数，避免通过填充数的绕路，从而得到尖锐的渐近度量熵界。作为应用，建立了有界积分概率度量的二分法：每个 IPM 要么可估计，要么不能在因子 c<3 内弱评估，而 3-弱评估总是成立，解决了 Aiyer et al. (ICML 2026) 的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决实值函数类学习理论中尺度依赖性的精确刻画问题，特别是回答 Anthony & Bartlett (1999) 中关于控制可学习性精确尺度的疑问，并否证 Phil Long 关于 2 倍乘法因子间隙不可避免的猜想。同时解决 Alon et al. (1997) 和 Rudelson & Vershynin (2006) 关于度量熵界的开放问题，以及 Aiyer et al. (ICML 2026) 关于积分概率度量评估性的开放问题。

Method: 核心技术是直接绑定经验 ℓ∞ 覆盖数，避免传统的通过填充数（packing numbers）的间接路径。利用这种直接方法，在脂肪破碎尺度 γ 下得到更紧的渐近度量熵界：在尺度 γ/2 处获得 O(log² n) 界，在尺度 2γ 处获得 O(log n) 界，并证明前者有时是紧的。

Result: 建立了实值函数类的尺度敏感的 PAC 学习基本定理推广：对于每个有界实值类 C 和 γ>0，以下三者等价——尺度 γ 处的均匀收敛、尺度 γ/2 处的半空间不可知学习性、以及每个 γ'>γ 处脂肪破碎维数的有限性。获得了尖锐的渐近度量熵界（O(log² n) 在 γ/2 处，O(log n) 在 2γ 处）。建立了有界积分概率度量的评估性二分法：每个 IPM 要么可估计，要么不能在因子 c<3 内弱评估，而 3-弱评估总成立。

Conclusion: 解决了机器学习理论中关于实值函数类可学习性精确尺度的长期开放问题，改进了已知上界，否证了广泛持有的猜想。该工作不仅提供了理论上的精确刻画，而且通过积分概率度量的应用展示了其实用价值，同时留下了关于定量样本复杂度和可评估性的若干开放问题供后续研究。

Abstract: We study the optimal scale at which real-valued function classes exhibit uniform convergence and learnability. Our main result establishes a scale-sensitive generalization of the fundamental theorem of PAC learning: for every bounded real-valued class and every $γ>0$, uniform convergence at scale $γ$, agnostic learnability at scale $γ/2$, and finiteness of the fat-shattering dimension at every scale $γ'>γ$ are equivalent. This resolves a question by Anthony and Bartlett (Cambridge Univ. Press 1999) on the precise scales governing learnability, refuting a conjecture attributed there to Phil Long that a multiplicative 2-factor gap is unavoidable, and improves the upper bounds of Bartlett and Long (JCSS 1998), which incur such a loss.
  The key technical ingredient is a direct bound on empirical $\ell_\infty$ covering numbers, avoiding the standard detour through packing numbers. As a consequence, we obtain sharp asymptotic metric-entropy bounds in terms of the fat-shattering scale $γ$: an $O(\log^2 n)$ bound holds already at scale $γ/2$, while an $O(\log n)$ bound holds at scale $2γ$. We further show that the $O(\log^2 n)$ bound is sometimes tight. These results resolve open questions by Alon et al. (JACM 1997) and Rudelson and Vershynin (Ann. of Math. 2006).
  As an application, we establish a sharp dichotomy for bounded integral probability metrics: every such IPM is either estimable or cannot be weakly evaluated within any multiplicative factor $c<3$, while $3$-weak evaluability always holds, resolving an open question from Aiyer et al. (ICML 2026). We also highlight several open questions on quantitative sample complexity and evaluability.

</details>


### [197] [Polyhedral Instability Governs Regret in Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13692)
*Yuetai Li,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Kaiyuan Zheng,Luyao Niu,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Linda Bushnell,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究组合动作在线决策问题的遗憾界，提出由多面体不稳定性（区域切换次数RS_T）驱动的遗憾上界Regret_T = Θ(√((1+RS_T)T log V_max))，并在在线次模-凹博弈中简化为排列切换次数SC_T，实验验证了低不稳定性场景在实际问题中的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线组合决策问题常通过凸松弛转化为在线凸优化（OCO）问题，但现有遗憾界未能充分利用问题的组合结构特性。本文旨在揭示遗憾与多面体区域切换次数之间的本质关系，从而统一专家学习与维度依赖的OCO遗憾界。

Method: 提出"多面体不稳定性"概念，量化在线算法遍历不同线性区域（多面体胞腔）的次数。在全信息反馈和固定划分假设下，推导遗憾上界为Θ(√((1+RS_T)T log V_max))；针对在线次模-凹博弈，利用Lovász凸化将问题转化为排列切换计数SC_T，得到相应的遗憾界。

Result: 理论上证明了遗憾与区域切换次数RS_T的平方根关系，实现了从专家学习（RS_T=0时退化为O(√(T log n))）到维度依赖OCO（RS_T大时逼近O(√(dT))）的平滑插值。在最短路径、影响力最大化等组合问题上实验验证了理论预测，并发现低不稳定性场景在实际中确实存在。

Conclusion: 多面体不稳定性是刻画组合在线决策问题遗憾的核心量，为设计更高效的在线算法提供了新视角，无需显式枚举所有动作即可利用问题的结构稀疏性。

Abstract: Many online decision problems over combinatorial actions are addressed via convex relaxations, leading to online convex optimization with piecewise linear objectives and induced polyhedral structure. We show that regret in such problems is governed by \emph{polyhedral instability}: the number of changes of the active region. Under full information feedback and fixed partition assumptions, if $\mathrm{RS}_T$ denotes the number of region switches and $V_{\max}$ the maximum number of vertices per region, we prove $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{RS}_T)\,T\,\log V_{\max}})$ interpolating between experts-like and dimension-dependent OCO rates. For online submodular--concave games under Lovász convexification, this reduces to the permutation-switch count $\mathrm{SC}_T$, yielding the matching rate $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{SC}_T)\,T\,\log n})$. Experiments on synthetic and real combinatorial problems (shortest path, influence maximization) validate the predicted scaling and indicate that low-instability regimes can arise in practice without explicit enumeration of actions.

</details>


### [198] [MILM: Large Language Models for Multimodal Irregular Time Series with Informative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13711)
*Hsing-Huan Chung,Shijun Li,Yoav Wald,Xing Han,Suchi Saria,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MILM模型，将多模态不规则时间序列表示为XML格式的三元组，通过两阶段微调LLM（先训练采样模式预测，再联合建模采样模式和观测值），在多个EHR数据集上取得最佳性能，并验证了采样模式本身的预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态不规则时间序列（MITS）中包含异步、非均匀采样的异构数值和文本数据，其不规则的时间间隔和通道模式本身携带预测信号，而现有方法难以有效融合这些异质信息。LLM具备处理文本和数值的预训练知识，但其如何适配MITS的异步不规则特性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将MITS表示为时间排序的XML格式三元组，采用两阶段微调策略：第一阶段在值被遮蔽的MITS上训练，使模型仅从采样模式中学习预测；第二阶段在全量MITS上训练，联合建模采样模式和观测值。两阶段模型称为MILM-2S，单阶段直接微调版本称为MILM-Direct。

Result: MILM-2S和MILM-Direct在多个EHR数据集上分别取得平均最佳和次佳性能。值遮蔽评估证实采样模式携带预测信号，且MILM-2S能有效利用这些信号。在引入的值缺失评估中，MILM-2S相比MILM-Direct优势更大。对于MILM-2S，保留值缺失观测的时间和通道信息可进一步改善院内死亡率预测。

Conclusion: 将MITS转化为XML格式以适配LLM是有效的，两阶段训练策略能分别捕捉采样模式和观测值的预测信号，且在值缺失场景下更具鲁棒性。该工作为利用LLM处理医疗等多模态不规则时序数据提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal irregular time series (MITS) consist of asynchronous and irregularly sampled observations from heterogeneous numerical and textual channels. In healthcare, for example, patients' electronic health records (EHR) include irregular lab measurements and clinical notes. The irregular timing and channel patterns of observations carry predictive signal alongside the numerical values and textual content. LLMs are natural candidates for processing such heterogeneous data, given their extensive pretrained knowledge spanning textual and numerical domains. We introduce MILM (Multimodal Irregular time series Language Model), which represents MITS as time-ordered triplets in Extensible Markup Language (XML) format and fine-tunes an LLM through a two-stage strategy for MITS classification. The first stage trains on value-redacted MITS to predict from sampling patterns alone, and the second stage trains on full MITS to jointly model sampling patterns and observed values. Our two-stage model (MILM-2S) and its single-stage counterpart (MILM-Direct) achieve the best and second-best average performance on multiple EHR datasets. Further value redaction evaluations confirm that sampling patterns carry predictive signal and that MILM-2S learns to exploit them. In the value pending evaluation we introduce, where some values are unavailable at prediction time, MILM-2S outperforms MILM-Direct by a larger margin compared to standard evaluation. For MILM-2S, preserving the time and channel of value-pending observations as additional sampling information further improves in-hospital mortality prediction.

</details>


### [199] [Tight Sample Complexity Bounds for Entropic Best Policy Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13717)
*Amer Essakine,Claire Vernade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对熵风险度量下的有限时域风险敏感强化学习中的最优策略识别问题，通过提出基于KL探索奖励的前向模型算法，结合指数效用函数的平滑性导出了更紧的浓度界限，并设计新的停止规则，将样本复杂度从已知的O(e^{2|β|H})下界改进至与下界Ω(e^{|β|H})匹配，弥合了指数时域依赖中的指数因子差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感强化学习中，最优策略识别问题存在一个指数时域依赖的常数差距：下界为Ω(e^{|β|H})，而现有最优上界仅为O(e^{2|β|H})。作者试图弥合这一差距，找出额外指数因子的来源并提出改进方法。

Method: 作者重新审视了该问题的分析，提出了一种基于前向模型的算法，该算法构建在KL散度探索奖励之上并适配熵准则。关键技术包括：(1)利用指数效用函数的平滑性质导出更尖锐的浓度界限；(2)提出一种新的停止规则以充分利用这种紧致性。

Result: 新算法的样本复杂度与已知下界Ω(e^{|β|H})匹配，消除了原先存在于下界和上界之间的额外指数因子O(e^{|β|H})。

Conclusion: 通过更紧的浓度控制和自适应停止规则，可以有效弥合风险敏感强化学习中策略识别问题的指数时域依赖差距，实现样本最优复杂度。

Abstract: We study best-policy identification for finite-horizon risk-sensitive reinforcement learning under the entropic risk measure. Recent work established a constant gap in the exponential horizon dependence between lower and upper bounds on the number of samples required to identify an approximately optimal policy. Precisely, known lower bounds scale in $Ω(e^{|β| H})$ where $H$ is the horizon of the MDP, while the state-of-the-art upper bound achieves at best $O(e^{2|β| H})$ (arXiv:2506.00286v2) using a generative model. We show that this extra exponential factor can be traced to overly loose concentration control for exponential utilities. To close this open gap, we revisit the analysis of this problem through a forward-model based algorithm building on KL-based exploration bonuses that we adapt to the entropic criterion. The improvement we get is due to two main novel technical innovations. We leverage the smoothness properties of the exponential utility to derive sharper concentration bounds, and we propose a new stopping rule that exploits further this tightness to obtain a sample complexity that matches the lower bound.

</details>


### [200] [Distinguishing performance gains from learning when using generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13731)
*Lixiang Yan,Samuel Greiff,Jason M. Lodge,Dragan Gašević*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，虽然生成式人工智能在教育中能够提升学习者的表现，但它未能促进高质量的深度学习所需的深度认知和元认知加工过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI虽被广泛用于教育并提升学习表现，但存在忽视深层认知和元认知加工的问题，可能限制高质量学习效果。

Method: 基于文献分析和理论论证，批判性地审视生成式AI在教育中的应用现状及其对深度学习的影响。

Result: 生成式AI的使用未能促进深度认知和元认知加工过程，不利于高质量学习。

Conclusion: 需重新设计AI教育工具，以更好地支持深度学习而非仅提升表面表现。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly being integrated into education, where it can boost learners' performance. However, these uses do not promote the deep cognitive and metacognitive processing that are required for high-quality learning.

</details>


### [201] [Learning POMDP World Models from Observations with Language-Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13740)
*Valentin Six,Frederik Panse,Mathis Fajeau,Lancelot Da Costa,Mridul Sharma,Alfonso Amayuelas,Tim Z. Xiao,David Hyland,Philipp Hennig,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pinductor方法，利用LLM先验知识从少量观测-动作轨迹中迭代推断POMDP世界模型，无需假设隐藏状态访问即可匹配甚至超越现有方法的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中学习世界模型需要大量环境交互，成本高昂。作者希望利用语言模型的先验知识减少这种代价昂贵的交互，实现样本高效的世界模型学习。

Method: Pinductor：LLM从少量观测-动作轨迹中提出候选POMDP模型，并基于信念似然分数迭代优化这些模型，无需访问隐藏状态即可进行学习。

Result: Pinductor在性能上与需要隐藏状态访问权限的LLM-based POMDP学习方法持平，在样本效率上显著超越表格型POMDP基线。性能随LLM能力提升而提升，且在环境语义信息被屏蔽时性能优雅退化。

Conclusion: 语言模型先验可作为部分可观测环境下样本高效世界模型学习的实用工具，为通用智能体在真实环境中应用迈进一步。

Abstract: Whether navigating a building, operating a robot, or playing a game, an agent that acts effectively in an environment must first learn an internal model of how that environment works. Partially-observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) provide a flexible modeling class for such internal world models, but learning them from observation-action trajectories alone is challenging and typically requires extensive environment interaction. We ask whether language-model priors can reduce costly interaction by leveraging prior knowledge, and introduce \emph{Pinductor} (POMDP-inductor): an LLM proposes candidate POMDP models from a few observation-action trajectories and iteratively refines them to optimize a belief-based likelihood score. Despite using strictly less information, \emph{Pinductor} matches the performance and sample efficiency of LLM-based POMDP learning methods that assume privileged access to the hidden state, while significantly surpassing the sample efficiency of tabular POMDP baselines. Further results show that performance scales with LLM capability and degrades gracefully as semantic information about the environment is withheld. Together, these results position language-model priors as a practical tool for sample-efficient world-model learning under partial observability, and a step toward generalist agents in real-world environments. Code is available at https://github.com/atomresearch/pinductor.

</details>


### [202] [GHGbench: A Unified Multi-Entity, Multi-Task Benchmark for Carbon Emission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13743)
*Yifan Duan,Siyuan Zheng,Lihuan Li,Chao Xue,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GHGbench，一个面向公司和建筑级别的温室气体排放预测的统一开放数据集与基准，包含公司级3.2万+记录和建筑级49万+记录，定义了标准化的数据划分与评估流程，揭示了建筑排放预测更难、分布外迁移差距远大于模型间差距等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前实体级碳排放预测的开放数据集和基准在获取途径、规模、粒度和评估方式上均呈现零散状态，缺乏统一规范的标准，阻碍了该领域的系统研究与发展。

Method: 构建双轨数据集：公司轨含3.2万+条公司-年记录（1.2万+企业，含Scope 1+2及Scope 3披露与财务/行业信号），建筑轨整合13个开源来源的49万+条建筑-年记录（统一模式，覆盖26个都会区，含气候协变量与多模态遥感嵌入）。定义规范数据划分（分布内与跨区域/城市迁移为主任务，时间留出与短期预测为辅），基线涵盖梯度提升树、表格基础模型、MLP、FT-Transformer及多模态融合，辅以LLM面板，采用多种子配对bootstrap检验评估。

Result: 三项基准级发现：(i) 建筑排放预测在结构上比公司排放更难；(ii) 分布内到分布外的迁移差距远超模型间差距；表格基础模型首次在配对bootstrap检验中显著优于调优树模型；(iii) 多模态遥感嵌入在表格泛化失效时提供有效帮助。同时暴露了灾难性城市迁移和行业因子查找天花板等系统性失败模式。

Conclusion: GHGbench为碳排放预测领域提供了首个统一、标准化的大规模开放基准，揭示了当前方法的局限性，并为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Open datasets and benchmarks for entity-level carbon-emission prediction remain fragmented across access, scale, granularity, and evaluation. We introduce GHGbench, an open dataset and benchmark for company- and building-level greenhouse-gas prediction. The company track contains 32,000+ company-year records from 12,000+ firms with Scope 1+2 and Scope 3 disclosures and financial/sectoral signals; the building track harmonises 491,591 building-year records from 13 open sources into a single schema across 26 metropolitan areas (10 U.S., 15 Australian, 1 Singaporean), with climate covariates and multimodal remote-sensing embeddings. GHGbench defines canonical splits with in-distribution and cross-region/city transfer as primary tasks and temporal hold-out plus short-horizon forecasting as supplementary appendix evidence; headline baselines span gradient-boosted trees, a tabular foundation model, MLP, FT-Transformer, and multimodal fusion, with an LLM panel as auxiliary, all evaluated under multi-seed paired-bootstrap tests. Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) building emissions are structurally harder than company emissions; (ii) the in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap dwarfs any within-model gap across both the company track and the building track, and a tabular foundation model is, to our knowledge, the first baseline to open a paired-bootstrap-significant gap over tuned trees on a multi-city building-emissions task; (iii) multimodal remote-sensing embeddings help precisely where tabular generalisation breaks. GHGbench also exposes catastrophic city transfer and the sector-factor lookup ceiling as systematic failure modes. Code and reconstruction recipes are available at GHGbench.

</details>


### [203] [Min Generalized Sliced Gromov Wasserstein: A Scalable Path to Gromov Wasserstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13753)
*Ashkan Shahbazi,Xinran Liu,Ping He,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出min-GSGW，一种使用可表达广义切片器的高效Gromov-Wasserstein切片求解方法，通过学习耦合的非线性切片器在原始空间直接优化GW目标，具有刚体运动不变性，在几何匹配任务中大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Gromov-Wasserstein求解器计算成本高昂，难以应用于大规模几何匹配任务；同时，传统切片GW方法使用的切片器表达能力有限。

Method: 提出min-GSGW框架：1）将广义切片器引入切片GW框架；2）利用学习到的耦合非线性切片器将输入测度投影到兼容空间，在投影域构造单调耦合并提升为原始空间的传输计划；3）直接在原始空间中最小化GW目标值；4）提出amortized变体，用学习到的切片器替代逐实例优化。

Result: 在动物网格匹配、马网格插值和ShapeNet部件迁移实验中，min-GSGW在显著低于现有GW求解器的计算成本下，产生了有意义的几何对应和GW目标值。

Conclusion: min-GSGW兼具GW的几何匹配能力和切片方法的计算效率，刚体运动不变性使其特别适用于几何匹配和形状分析任务，amortized变体进一步提升了泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose min Generalized Sliced Gromov--Wasserstein (min-GSGW), a sliced formulation for the Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) problem using expressive generalized slicers. The key idea is to learn coupled nonlinear slicers that assign compatible push-forward values to both input measures, so that monotone coupling in the projected domain lifts to a transport plan evaluated against the GW objective in the original spaces. The resulting plan induces a GW objective value, and min-GSGW minimizes this cost directly in the original spaces. We further show that min-GSGW is rigid-motion invariant, a crucial property for geometric matching and shape analysis tasks. Our contributions are threefold: 1) we introduce generalized slicers into the sliced GW framework, 2) we construct a slicing-based efficient GW transport plan; and 3) we develop an amortized variant that replaces per-instance optimization with a learned slicer for unseen input pairs. We perform experiments on animal mesh matching, horse mesh interpolation, and ShapeNet part transfer. Results show that min-GSGW produces meaningful geometric correspondences and GW objective values at substantially lower computational cost than existing GW solvers.

</details>


### [204] [Fast and effective algorithms for fair clustering at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13759)
*Claudio Mantuano,Manuel Kammermann,Philipp Baumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种公平聚类通用框架，通过三种启发式方法精确控制聚类成本与公平性之间的权衡，在基准数据集上优于现有方法，且支持大规模数据集高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类在公平敏感领域（如客户、员工、学生分组）广泛应用，但现有方法难以有效管理聚类成本与公平性之间的权衡，要么无法扩展到大数据集，要么扩展时解质量低。

Method: 提出一个通用公平聚类框架，基于该框架设计了三种启发式方法：第一种注重解质量和约束灵活性；第二种在保持高质量的同时提升可扩展性；第三种面向最大可扩展性，可在数秒内处理百万级对象实例。

Result: 在基准数据集上的综合数值实验中，三种启发式方法均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了公平聚类中成本-公平性权衡难以精确控制的问题，同时兼顾了解质量和可扩展性，代码已公开可复现。

Abstract: Clustering is an unsupervised machine learning task that consists of identifying groups of similar objects. It has numerous applications and is increasingly used in fairness-sensitive domains where objects represent individuals, such as customers, employees, or students. We address a fair clustering problem in which objects belong to protected groups. The problem consists of partitioning the objects into a predefined number of clusters while attaining a user-defined target level of fairness, meaning that each protected group is sufficiently represented in each cluster. The objective is to minimize the clustering cost, defined as the sum of squared Euclidean distances between the objects and the centers of their clusters. Since clustering cost and fairness are generally in conflict, managing the trade-off between them is essential in practical applications. Existing methods provide limited control over this trade-off and either fail to scale to large datasets or, when they scale, produce low-quality solutions. We propose a general framework for fair clustering that provides precise control over the cost-fairness trade-off and introduce three heuristics based on it. The first heuristic focuses on solution quality and the flexibility to incorporate additional constraints, the second improves scalability while retaining high solution quality, and the third is designed for maximum scalability, producing solutions for instances with millions of objects in seconds. The proposed heuristics outperform existing approaches in comprehensive numerical experiments on benchmark datasets. The source code of our heuristics and instructions for reproducing the experiments are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [205] [Attention Once Is All You Need: Efficient Streaming Inference with Stateful Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13784)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flash Queries，一种面向流式工作负载的数据驱动推理引擎。通过有状态会话（持久化KV缓存增量更新）将prefill移出查询关键路径，使查询延迟降至O(|q|)且与累积上下文规模无关，并利用GPU空闲周期预计算查询答案。在流式市场数据基准测试中，相比vLLM、SGLang等引擎实现最高5.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer推理引擎采用请求驱动模式，每次查询都需支付O(n)的prefill代价。在流式工作负载中，数据持续到达、查询不断增长，这种代价难以承受。亟需一种将prefill移出查询关键路径的新计算模型。

Method: 提出基于有状态会话的数据驱动计算模型：1) 持久化KV缓存随新数据增量推进，prefill移出关键路径，查询延迟降至O(|q|)；2) Flash Queries模式，利用数据到达间的空闲GPU周期预计算已注册查询并缓存答案；3) 多租户连续批处理调度器，包含cell-budget准入控制和前缀感知的分组prefill，支持数十个有状态会话共享单GPU并保持完整二次自注意力。

Result: 在流式市场数据基准测试中，参考实现相比vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、llama.cpp等传统推理引擎实现最高5.9倍加速，且查询延迟随累积上下文增长保持恒定。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的有状态会话模型能够从根本上解决流式场景下传统推理引擎的prefill瓶颈，将查询延迟与上下文规模解耦，同时利用空闲周期实现预计算，为流式大模型推理提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Conventional transformer inference engines are request-driven, paying an O(n) prefill cost on every query. In streaming workloads, where data arrives continuously and queries probe an ever-growing context, this cost is prohibitive. We introduce a data-driven computational model centred on stateful sessions: a persistent KV cache advanced incrementally as new data arrives, so prefill is moved off the critical path and query latency becomes O(|q|), independent of accumulated context size. Building on this, Flash Queries reclaim idle GPU cycles between data arrivals to pre-evaluate registered questions and return cached answers before the user asks, a pattern that is structurally impossible in stateless engines because they discard intermediate state between requests. A multi-tenant continuous-batching scheduler with cell-budget admission and prefix-aware grouped prefill lets dozens of stateful sessions coexist on a single GPU while preserving full quadratic self-attention. On streaming market-data benchmarks the reference implementation achieves up to 5.9x speedup over conventional inference engines (vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, llama.cpp), holding query latency constant as accumulated context grows.

</details>


### [206] [Interpretable Machine Learning for Antepartum Prediction of Pregnancy-Associated Thrombotic Microangiopathy Using Routine Longitudinal Laboratory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13786)
*Chuanchuan Sun,Zhen Yu,Qin Fan,Qingchao Chen,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习分析纵向实验室检验数据，构建了妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）的早期风险预测模型。梯度提升模型在留出测试集上表现良好（AUROC 0.872, AUPRC 0.883），发现第6周胱抑素C可作为早期监测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）虽罕见但危及生命，其早期风险预测极具挑战性。传统单变量或规则性方法难以捕捉该疾病多维、时间依赖的实验室异常信号，这些信号常被妊娠期生理变化掩盖，因此需要更先进的机器学习方法从纵向临床检验数据中提取潜在风险特征。

Method: 回顾性纳入300例妊娠（142例P-TMA，158例对照），保留146个纵向实验室预测变量。采用分层抽样划分80%训练集和20%留出测试集，评估逻辑回归、支持向量机（RBF核）、随机森林、极端随机树和梯度提升五种算法。通过交叉验证平均AUROC选出梯度提升模型，在训练集上重新拟合后在留出测试集上评估，并进行全局特征重要性和分布模式的可解释性分析。

Result: 梯度提升为最优模型，在留出测试集上AUROC为0.872（95% CI: 0.769-0.952），AUPRC为0.883（95% CI: 0.780-0.959），灵敏度0.750，特异度0.812。

Conclusion: 常规临床实验室检验中的纵向数据包含可用于P-TMA早期风险预测的信息性和临床合理性信号，其中第6周胱抑素C水平具有作为早期监测指标的潜力。

Abstract: Background: Pregnancy-associated thrombotic microangiopathy (P-TMA) is rare but life-threatening. Early risk prediction before overt clinical presentation remains challenging, as the associated laboratory abnormalities are subtle, multidimensional, and frequently masked by common physiological changes such as gestational thrombocytopenia and pregnancy-related proteinuria, thus overlapping heavily with benign obstetric and renal conditions. This complexity is poorly captured by univariate or rule-based approaches; however, it is addressable by machine learning, which can extract latent, time-dependent risk signatures from longitudinal clinical tests. Methods: This retrospective study included 300 pregnancies comprising 142 P-TMA cases and 158 controls. After exclusion of identifiers and non-informative variables, 146 longitudinal laboratory predictors were retained. Participants were divided into a training cohort (80%) and a held-out test cohort (20%) using stratified sampling. Five algorithms were evaluated: logistic regression, support vector machine with radial basis function kernel, random forest, extra trees, and gradient boosting. The final model was selected by mean cross-validated AUROC, refitted on the full training cohort, and evaluated once in the held-out test cohort. Interpretability analyses examined global feature importance and distributional patterns of leading predictors. Results: Gradient boosting was prespecified by cross-validation in the training cohort. The model achieved an AUROC of 0.872 (95% CI: 0.769-0.952) and an AUPRC of 0.883 (95% CI: 0.780-0.959) in a held-out test cohort, with sensitivity of 0.750 and specificity of 0.812. Conclusions: Longitudinal clinical laboratory tests obtained during routine care contained informative and clinically plausible signals for P-TMA risk. Notably, cystatin C at week 6 showed promise as an early monitoring indicator.

</details>


### [207] [Force-Aware Neural Tangent Kernels for Scalable and Robust Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13788)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Zachary Weller-Davies,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Shikha Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分块特征空间后验方差筛选的线性缩放采集框架，结合力感知神经正切核（NTK），实现了可扩展、力感知且对分布偏移鲁棒的主动学习，适用于机器学习原子间势能模型的微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主动学习方法在应用于机器学习原子间势能模型（MLIPs）时面临三大挑战：难以扩展到大规模候选池、无法有效利用能量-力监督信号、以及当候选池分布相对于目标分布存在偏移时鲁棒性不足。本文旨在同时解决这些挑战。

Method: 1) 提出基于分块特征空间后验方差筛选的线性缩放采集框架，通过避免物化候选集和训练集核矩阵，实现对约20万结构的快速筛选；2) 将神经正切核（NTK）扩展至力感知场景，通过混合参数-坐标导数推导出力NTK及联合能量-力NTK，作为向量场预测的相似性度量。

Result: 在OC20数据集上，联合能量-力NTK实现了最低的能量和力MAE及RMSE；在T1x、PMechDB和RGD基准测试中，力NTK方法在保持竞争力的同时显著优于基于委员会的方法；在T1x的受控候选池偏移实验中，基于预训练MLIP嵌入和NTK的采集保持鲁棒，而委员会方法表现出更高方差。

Conclusion: 单个预训练的MLIP即可实现可扩展、力感知且对分布鲁棒的主动学习，用于基础模型的微调。

Abstract: Active learning for machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) must address several challenges to be practical: scaling to large candidate pools, leveraging energy-force supervision, and maintaining robustness when candidate pools are biased relative to the target distribution. In this work, we jointly address these challenges. We first introduce a linearly scaling acquisition framework based on chunked feature-space posterior-variance shortlisting. By avoiding materialisation of the candidate and train set kernels, this approach enables screening of ~200k structures within hours and applies broadly to acquisition strategies that score candidates based on molecular similarity metrics. We then extend the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) to a force-aware setting via mixed parameter-coordinate derivatives, yielding a force NTK and a joint energy-force NTK that provide natural similarity metrics for vector-field prediction. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the joint energy-force NTK on the OC20 dataset, where force-aware acquisition is crucial: it achieves the lowest energy and force MAE and RMSE across all metrics and distribution splits. Across T1x, PMechDB, and RGD benchmarks, our force NTK methods remain competitive with established baselines while being significantly more efficient than committee-based approaches. Under a controlled candidate-pool shift case study on T1x, acquisition based on pretrained MLIP embeddings and NTKs remains robust, whereas committee-based methods exhibit higher variance. Overall, these results show that a single pretrained MLIP can enable scalable, force-aware, and distribution-robust active learning for foundation-model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [208] [Provable Quantization with Randomized Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13810)
*Ying Feng,Piotr Indyk,Michael Kapralov,Dmitry Krachun,Boris Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种结合随机Hadamard变换和抖动量化的向量量化方法，证明了该方法无偏且均方误差界渐近匹配真正随机旋转矩阵的效果，其中TurboQuant的抖动版本在b比特/坐标下达到(π√3/2+o(1))·4^{-b}的MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集随机旋转矩阵有理论保证但需Θ(d²)时间，随机Hadamard变换将代价降至O(d log d)但其离散结构导致分析困难、压缩保证较弱或仅凭经验。作者希望设计一种既能利用Hadamard变换的计算效率、又能获得与密集随机旋转相当的理论保证的量化方法。

Method: 采用抖动量化（dithered quantization）与单次随机Hadamard变换结合：对输入向量应用HD变换，减去随机标量偏移后再进行标量量化，以可忽略的额外代价注入额外随机性。具体分析了抖动版TurboQuant的性能。

Result: 证明该方法无偏，且均方误差界为(π√3/2 + o(1))·4^{-b}（b比特/坐标），其中o(1)项在所有单位向量和所有维度上随着量化级数增长而一致消失，渐近匹配真正随机旋转矩阵的理论性能。

Conclusion: 抖动量化与单次随机Hadamard变换结合能够在保持计算效率（O(d log d)）的同时，获得与密集随机旋转矩阵同等的渐近理论保证，为实际应用（如相似性搜索、联邦学习、KV缓存压缩）提供了高效且可理论保障的量化方案。

Abstract: Vector quantization via random projection followed by scalar quantization is a fundamental primitive in machine learning, with applications ranging from similarity search to federated learning and KV cache compression. While dense random rotations yield clean theoretical guarantees, they require $Θ(d^2)$ time. The randomized Hadamard transform $HD$ reduces this cost to $O(d \log d)$, but its discrete structure complicates analysis and leads to weaker or purely empirical compression guarantees.
  In this work, we study a variant of this approach: dithered quantization with a single randomized Hadamard transform. Specifically, the quantizer applies $HD$ to the input vector and subtracts a random scalar offset before quantizing, injecting additional randomness at negligible cost. We prove that this approach is unbiased and provides mean squared error bounds that asymptotically match those achievable with truly random rotation matrices. In particular, we prove that a dithered version of TurboQuant achieves mean squared error $\bigl(π\sqrt{3}/2 + o(1)\bigr) \cdot 4^{-b}$ at $b$ bits per coordinate, where the $o(1)$ term vanishes uniformly over all unit vectors and all dimensions as the number of quantization levels grows.

</details>


### [209] [Uncertainty-Driven Anomaly Detection for Psychotic Relapse Using Smartwatches: Forecasting and Multi-Task Learning Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13816)
*Nikolaos Tsalkitzis,Panagiotis P. Filntisis,Petros Maragos,Niki Efthymiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于智能手表的日常精神病复发检测框架：一种通过预测心脏动力学并检测预测与观测特征之间的偏差，另一种采用多任务学习融合睡眠、运动和心脏信号并学习时间感知嵌入。两种框架均使用Transformer编码器并通过MLP集成估计不确定性来输出每日异常分数。通过后期融合策略将两者结合，在e-Prevention Grand Challenge数据集上相比竞赛冠军基线实现了8%的相对提升。消融研究表明，整合心脏、运动和睡眠等多种数字表型对于高保真检测精神病复发至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用数字表型技术实现对精神病复发的早期检测，通过被动连续监测行为和生理信号，替代传统依赖主观报告或偶发临床评估的方式，提高复发检测的及时性和客观性。

Method: 提出两个基于智能手表的检测框架：(1) 框架一：预测心脏动力学，计算预测与观测值之间的偏差作为异常指标；(2) 框架二：多任务学习融合睡眠、运动和心脏信号，学习时间感知嵌入并预测测量时序。两个框架均采用Transformer编码器，通过MLP集成估计预测不确定性以获得每日异常分数，最后通过后期融合策略将两个框架的异常信号整合为统一决策分数。

Result: 在2nd e-Prevention Grand Challenge数据集上，融合模型相比竞赛冠军基线实现了8%的相对提升。两个框架各自独立表现出强大的预测能力，且捕获了互补的生理特征。

Conclusion: 整合心脏、运动和睡眠等多种数字表型对于在真实环境中高保真检测精神病复发至关重要，多模态融合策略能够有效提升检测性能。

Abstract: Digital phenotyping enables continuous passive monitoring of behavior and physiology, offering a promising paradigm for early detection of psychotic relapse. In this work, we develop and systematically study two smartwatch-based frameworks for daily relapse detection. The first forecasts cardiac dynamics and flags deviations between predicted and observed features as indicators of abnormality. The second adopts a multi-task formulation that fuses sleep with motion and cardiac-derived signals, learning time-aware embeddings and predicting measurement timing. Both pipelines use Transformer encoders and output a daily anomaly score, derived from predictive uncertainty estimated via an ensemble of multilayer perceptrons to improve robustness to real-world wearable variability. While each framework independently demonstrates strong predictive power, we show that they capture complementary physiological signatures. Consequently, we propose a late-fusion strategy that synergistically combines the anomaly signals from both architectures into a unified decision score. We benchmark our methodology on the 2nd e-Prevention Grand Challenge dataset, where our fused model achieves a 8% relative improvement over the competition-winning baseline. Our results, supported by extensive ablation studies, suggest that the integration of diverse digital phenotypes, cardiac, motion, and sleep, is essential for the high-fidelity detection of psychotic relapse in real-world settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Think Twice, Act Once: Verifier-Guided Action Selection For Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12620)
*Nishad Singhi,Christian Bialas,Snehal Jauhri,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Marcus Rohrbach,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VeGAS框架，通过在测试时对MLLM生成的候选动作进行验证选择，无需修改基础策略即可提升具身智能体的鲁棒性和泛化能力，在Habitat和ALFRED基准上最高获得36%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）具身智能体在复杂现实任务中仍存在分布外场景下的脆弱性问题，直接使用MLLM作为验证器无法改善性能。

Method: 提出VeGAS框架：1）推理时采样多个候选动作；2）使用生成式验证器选择最可靠的行动；3）通过LLM驱动的数据合成策略自动构建多样化失败案例课程来训练验证器。

Result: 在Habitat和ALFRED环境的具身推理基准上，VeGAS一致性地提升泛化性能，在最具挑战性的多目标、长时域任务中相比强CoT基线获得最高36%的相对性能提升。

Conclusion: VeGAS通过测试时验证框架有效提升了MLLM具身智能体的鲁棒性，验证了显式验证步骤和自动数据合成策略对分布外泛化的重要性。

Abstract: Building generalist embodied agents capable of solving complex real-world tasks remains a fundamental challenge in AI. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of such agents through strong vision-language knowledge and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, yet remain brittle when faced with challenging out-of-distribution scenarios. To address this, we propose Verifier-Guided Action Selection (VegAS), a test-time framework designed to improve the robustness of MLLM-based embodied agents through an explicit verification step. At inference time, rather than committing to a single decoded action, VeGAS samples an ensemble of candidate actions and uses a generative verifier to identify the most reliable choice, without modifying the underlying policy. Crucially, we find that using an MLLM off-the-shelf as a verifier yields no improvement, motivating our LLM-driven data synthesis strategy, which automatically constructs a diverse curriculum of failure cases to expose the verifier to a rich distribution of potential errors at training time. Across embodied reasoning benchmarks spanning the Habitat and ALFRED environments, VeGAS consistently improves generalization, achieving up to a 36% relative performance gain over strong CoT baselines on the most challenging multi-object, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [211] [Macro-Action Based Multi-Agent Instruction Following through Value Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12655)
*Wo Wei Lin,Ethan Rathbun,Enrico Marchesini Xiang Zhi Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAVIC通过修正指令边界处的Bellman备份目标，解决了多智能体强化学习中外部自然语言指令中断宏动作导致的值估计不一致问题，在保持基础任务性能的同时实现高指令遵从性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多智能体强化学习需要适应中断持续行为并与长周期目标冲突的外部自然语言指令，但将奖励条件化在指令上会导致Bellman更新跨指令上下文耦合值估计，当指令中断宏动作时产生不一致的值。

Method: 提出宏动作值校正方法（MAVIC），在指令边界处修正Bellman备份：一方面校正传入的指令目标，另一方面恢复当前目标下的延续价值。与奖励塑形不同，MAVIC直接修改自举目标本身，使得在随机指令切换下能够实现统一策略内一致的值估计。

Result: 在日益复杂的协作多智能体环境中，MAVIC实现了高指令遵从性，同时保持了基础任务性能。

Conclusion: MAVIC提供了理论分析和actor-critic实现，有效解决了指令中断宏动作带来的值估计不一致问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) in real-world use cases may need to adapt to external natural language instructions that interrupt ongoing behavior and conflict with long-horizon objectives. However, conditioning rewards on instructions introduces a fundamental failure mode as Bellman updates couple value estimates across instruction contexts, leading to inconsistent values when instructions interrupt macro-actions. We propose Macro-Action Value Correction for Instruction Compliance (MAVIC), which corrects Bellman backups at instruction boundaries by correcting the incoming instruction objective and restoring the continuation value under the current objective. Unlike reward shaping, MAVIC modifies the bootstrapping target itself, enabling consistent value estimation under stochastic instruction switching within a unified policy. We provide theoretical analysis and an actor-critic implementation, and show that MAVIC achieves high instruction compliance while preserving base task performance in increasingly complex cooperative multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [212] [Revealing Interpretable Failure Modes of VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12674)
*Isha Chaudhary,Vedaant V Jain,Kavya Sachdeva,Sayan Ranu,Gagandeep Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REVELIO是一个系统化框架，通过结合多样性感知波束搜索和高斯过程Thompson采样策略，在指数级大的组合空间中高效发现VLMs中可解释的故障模式，并在自动驾驶和室内机器人领域揭示了新的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLMs在安全关键应用中表现出色，但在特定真实场景中会出现灾难性故障，而现有方法缺乏系统性地发现这些可解释故障模式的手段。

Method: 将故障模式定义为可解释、领域相关概念的组合；采用多样性感知波束搜索高效映射故障空间，结合高斯过程Thompson采样策略实现复杂故障模式的广泛探索。

Result: 在自动驾驶环境中，VLMs表现出薄弱的空间定位能力，忽视主要障碍物，导致模拟碰撞；在室内机器人任务中，VLMs要么忽略安全风险，要么过度保守产生误报，降低运行效率。

Conclusion: REVELIO能够识别结构化和可解释的故障模式，为VLMs的安全改进提供可操作的洞见，有助于提升VLMs在安全关键应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used in safety-critical applications because of their broad reasoning capabilities and ability to generalize with minimal task-specific engineering. Despite these advantages, they can exhibit catastrophic failures in specific real-world situations, constituting failure modes.
  We introduce REVELIO, a framework for systematically uncovering interpretable failure modes in VLMs. We define a failure mode as a composition of interpretable, domain-relevant concepts-such as pedestrian proximity or adverse weather conditions-under which a target VLM consistently behaves incorrectly. Identifying such failures requires searching over an exponentially large discrete combinatorial space. To address this challenge, REVELIO combines two search procedures: a diversity-aware beam search that efficiently maps the failure landscape, and a Gaussian-process Thompson Sampling strategy that enables broader exploration of complex failure modes.
  We apply REVELIO to autonomous driving and indoor robotics domains, uncovering previously unreported vulnerabilities in state-of-the-art VLMs. In driving environments, the models often demonstrate weak spatial grounding and fail to account for major obstructions, leading to recommendations that would result in simulated crashes. In indoor robotics tasks, VLMs either miss safety hazards or behave excessively conservatively, producing false alarms and reducing operational efficiency. By identifying structured and interpretable failure modes, REVELIO offers actionable insights that can support targeted VLM safety improvements.

</details>


### [213] [Learning Transferable Latent User Preferences for Human-Aligned Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12682)
*Alina Hyk,Sandhya Saisubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLIPR是一个通过少量对话交互从用户处学习可迁移的潜在偏好规则，并将其应用于LLM推理以提升对齐性的框架，在多个数据集和用户研究中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为推理模块时往往无法产生与人类对齐的解决方案，现有方法要么需要大量用户交互，要么无法跨任务泛化潜在偏好，缺乏实用性。

Method: 提出CLIPR框架，通过对话式学习从少量交互中推断用户潜在偏好，将其表示为可迁移的自然语言规则，并通过自适应反馈迭代优化，应用于分布内和分布外的模糊任务。

Result: 在三个数据集和用户研究中，CLIPR在提升对齐性和降低推理成本方面一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过有限交互学习可迁移的偏好规则是提升LLM对齐性的有效途径，CLIPR在实用性和泛化能力上具有优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as reasoning modules in many applications. While they are efficient in certain tasks, LLMs often struggle to produce human-aligned solutions. Human-aligned decision making requires accounting for both explicitly stated goals and latent user preferences that shape how ambiguous situations should be resolved. Existing approaches to incorporating such preferences either rely on extensive and repeated user interactions or fail to generalize latent preferences across tasks and contexts, limiting their practical applicability. We consider a setting in which an LLM is used for high-level reasoning and is responsible for inferring latent user preferences from limited interactions, which guides downstream decision making. We introduce CLIPR (Conversational Learning for Inferring Preferences and Reasoning), a framework that learns actionable, transferable natural language rules that represent latent user preferences from minimal conversational input. These rules are iteratively refined through adaptive feedback and applied to both in-distribution and out-of-distribution ambiguous tasks across multiple environments. Evaluations on three datasets and a user study show that CLIPR consistently outperforms existing methods in improving alignment and reducing inference costs.

</details>


### [214] [On the Size Complexity and Decidability of First-Order Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12691)
*Jens Classen,Daxin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了情景演算框架下三类可表达一阶逻辑推进行为（局部效应、正规动作、无环动作）的复杂度，证明了在合理假设下其推进规模呈多项式增长，且当知识库属于可判定片段（如二变量一阶逻辑或带常数的全称理论）时，推进保持在同一片段内，从而确保可判定性和实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识库推进（Progression）通常需要二阶逻辑，为求实际可用性需寻找一阶逻辑特例。已有工作识别了三类可一阶推进的动作类，但缺乏对推进规模的系统分析，而这对于实际应用至关重要。

Method: 在情景演算框架下，对局部效应、正规动作、无环动作这三类动作进行形式化分析，证明其一阶推进在合理假设下多项式增长；并进一步考察当知识库属于二变量一阶逻辑或带常数的全称理论等可判定片段时，推进的保片段性质。

Result: 1）三类动作的一阶推进规模呈多项式增长；2）当知识库属于二变量一阶逻辑或带常数的全称理论时，推进结果仍属于同一可判定片段。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了动作推理中推进规模分析的空白，从理论层面证明了局部效应、正规动作、无环动作的一阶推进具有实际可行性，为知识库动态更新的可判定性和高效实现提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Progression, the task of updating a knowledge base to reflect action effects, generally requires second-order logic. Identifying first-order special cases, by restricting either the knowledge base or action effects, has long been a central topic in reasoning about actions. It is known that local-effect, normal, and acyclic actions, three increasingly expressive classes, admit first-order progression. However, a systematic analysis of the size of such progressions, crucial for practical applications, has been missing. In this paper, using the framework of Situation Calculus, we show that under reasonable assumptions, first-order progression for these action classes grows only polynomially. Moreover, we show that when the KB belongs to decidable fragments such as two-variable first-order logic or universal theories with constants, the progression remains within the same fragment, ensuring decidability and practical applicability.

</details>


### [215] [DisaBench: A Participatory Evaluation Framework for Disability Harms in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12702)
*Eugenia Kim,Ioana Tanase,Christina Mallon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DisaBench，一个与残障人士共同设计的12类残障伤害分类体系、评估方法论及175条提示词数据集，发现残障伤害因类型而异、在非文本模态中会复合、术语驱动的伤害具有文化时间局限性，且标准安全评估只能捕捉显性失败而遗漏需要领域专业知识才能识别的细微伤害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型安全基准无法充分评估与残障相关的伤害，需要构建一个专门针对残障人群的、系统化的安全评估框架。

Method: 与残障人士和红队测试专家共同创建12类残障伤害分类体系；设计成对良性/对抗性提示的评估方法论，涵盖7个生活领域；构建包含175条提示词、525个提示-响应对的数据集，由4名具有残障生活经验的评估者进行标注。

Result: 伤害率因残障类型差异显著；非文本模态下伤害会复合；术语驱动的伤害具有文化和时间依赖性而非普遍可评估；标准安全评估能捕捉显性失败但遗漏仅领域专家可识别的细微伤害。

Conclusion: 残障伤害同时具有个人性、交叉性和社区定义性，无法脱离人的完整语境孤立评估，通用基准系统地遗漏了这类伤害。数据集、分类体系和方法论将通过Hugging Face和开源红队框架公开，可无缝集成到现有安全流程中。

Abstract: General-purpose safety benchmarks for large language models do not adequately evaluate disability-related harms. We introduce DisaBench: a taxonomy of twelve disability harm categories co-created with people with disabilities and red teaming experts, a taxonomy-driven evaluation methodology that pairs benign and adversarial prompts across seven life domains, and a dataset of 175 prompts with human-annotated labels on 525 prompt-response pairs. Annotation by four evaluators with lived disability experience reveals three findings: harm rates vary sharply by disability type and will compound in non-text modalities, terminology-driven harm is culturally and temporally bound rather than universally assessable, and standard safety evaluation catches overt failures while missing the subtle harms that only domain expertise can recognize. Disability harm is simultaneously personal, intersectional, and community-defined: it cannot be isolated from the full context of who a person is, and general-purpose benchmarks systematically miss it. We will release the dataset, taxonomy, and methodology via Hugging Face and an open-source red teaming framework for direct integration into existing safety pipelines with no additional infrastructure.

</details>


### [216] [CHAL: Council of Hierarchical Agentic Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12718)
*Tommaso Giovannelli,Griffin D. Kent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAL框架，将多智能体辩论从追求正确答案的任务转向可废止论证领域，通过图结构信念表示、贝叶斯启发的信念模式（CBS）和梯度驱动的动态机制，将元认知价值系统作为可配置超参数，实现结构化信念优化。实验表明，裁决者的价值系统决定辩论轨迹，委员会多样性优化所有参与者信念，框架具有良好的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体辩论方法存在结构限制：辩论产生鞅性质的信念轨迹、多数投票贡献了大部分收益、LLM出现置信度升级而非校准。作者认为辩论和辩证系统的真正价值不在于追求正确答案的任务，而在于每个立场都可能被更好的推理推翻的可废止领域。

Method: 提出Council of Hierarchical Agentic Language (CHAL)框架，每个智能体维护CHAL Belief Schema (CBS)——一种图结构信念表示，具有贝叶斯启发的架构，通过将信念论点的强度作为可微目标，利用梯度信息驱动的动态机制进行信念修正。元认知价值系统（涵盖认识论、逻辑学和伦理学）被提升为可配置超参数，控制智能体推理和裁决结果。

Result: 消融实验表明：裁决者的价值系统决定潜在信念空间中辩论的整体轨迹；委员会多样性优化所有参与者的信念；框架在广泛领域具有良好泛化性。CHAL是首个将多智能体辩论视为可废止领域上结构化信念优化的框架，其可审计的信念产物为可废止论证的专用评估套件奠定了基础。

Conclusion: CHAL框架证明了可废止论证作为信念优化引擎的有效性，其核心贡献在于将辩论从正确性竞赛重新定义为结构化信念优化过程，并产生可审计的信念产物，有助于构建推理和价值观透明、对齐且受人类监督的AI系统。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate has emerged as a promising approach for improving LLM reasoning on ground-truth tasks, yet current methodologies face certain structural limitations: debate tends to induce a martingale over belief trajectories, majority voting accounts for most observed gains, and LLMs exhibit confidence escalation rather than calibration across rounds. We argue that the genuine value of debate, and dialectic systems as a whole, lies not in ground-truth tasks but in defeasible domains, where every position can in principle be defeated by better reasoning. We present the Council of Hierarchical Agentic Language (CHAL), a multi-agent dialectic framework that treats defeasible argumentation as an engine for belief optimization. Each agent maintains a CHAL Belief Schema (CBS), a graph-structured belief representation with a Bayesian-inspired architecture, that facilitates belief revision through a gradient-informed dynamic mechanism by leveraging the strength of the belief's thesis as a differentiable objective. Meta-cognitive value systems spanning epistemology, logic, and ethics are elevated to configurable hyperparameters governing agent reasoning and adjudication outcomes. We provide a series of ablation experiments that demonstrate systematic and interpretable effects: the adjudicator's value system determines the debate's overall trajectories in latent belief space, council diversity refines beliefs for all participants, and the framework generalizes across broad fields. CHAL is, to our knowledge, the first framework to treat multi-agent debate as structured belief optimization over defeasible domains. Further, the auditable belief artifacts it produces establish the foundation for dedicated evaluation suites for defeasible argumentation, with broader implications for building AI systems whose reasoning and value commitments are transparent, aligned, and subject to human oversight.

</details>


### [217] [BEHAVE: A Hybrid AI Framework for Real-Time Modeling of Collective Human Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12730)
*Helene Malyutina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BEHAVE框架，将人类群体互动建模为复杂动力系统，通过连续行为场描述集体动力学，并基于运动微信号预测群体状态演变。框架包含定理和结构命题，支持从数据中学习、表征和预测集体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI行为模型局限于个体层面或事后检测，无法捕捉决定群体稳定或升级/崩溃的集体动力学。需要一种能将群体视为复杂动力系统的基础性新方法。

Method: 将群体互动建模为连续行为场，从运动学微信号（位置、速度、身体朝向、手势活动）构建有向交互图，聚合为行为场基。提出张力场、场基和临界性指数三个核心概念。使用神经模型实现感知与预测层。

Result: 在7人谈判场景上验证了工作流水线。框架可通过重新校准适用于 crowd 安全、危机团队动力学、教育和临床等多种场景。

Conclusion: BEHAVE为集体行为建模提供了严格的形式化框架，将群体视为复杂动力系统，具有跨场景迁移能力，超越了传统个体层面或事后分析的方法局限性。

Abstract: Existing AI systems for modeling human behavior operate at the level of individuals or detect events after they occur. As a result, they systematically fail to capture the collective dynamics that determine whether a group remains stable or transitions into escalation or breakdown. We propose a different foundation: a group of interacting humans constitutes a complex dynamical system in the precise mathematical sense, exhibiting emergence, nonlinearity, feedback loops, sensitivity near critical points, and phase transitions between qualitatively distinct regimes. The state of such a system is not located within any single participant; it is distributed across mutual influence loops and observable through the micro-dynamics of the body.
  We introduce BEHAVE (Behavioral Engine for Human Activity Vector Estimation), a formal framework that models collective dynamics as continuous behavioral fields defined over an interaction space derived from observable physical signals. Kinematic micro-signals (position, velocity, body orientation, gestural activity) are structured into a directed interaction graph and aggregated into a basis of behavioral fields capturing distinct, non-redundant axes of collective state. The framework rests on one theorem and two structural propositions characterizing the tension field, the field basis, and the criticality index. Perception and forecasting layers are implemented using neural models, enabling data-driven learning and approximation of system dynamics. BEHAVE is formulated as a computational system for learning, representing, and forecasting collective dynamics from data. A working pipeline is demonstrated on a 7-agent negotiation snapshot. The same fields, recalibrated, apply to crowd safety, crisis-team dynamics, education, and clinical contexts.

</details>


### [218] [State-Centric Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12755)
*Sungheon Jeong,Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出状态中心决策过程（SDP），一种让智能体在语言环境中通过构建自然语言谓词来生成状态空间、观测映射、转移和终止条件的运行时框架，在五个基准上取得最佳无训练结果，并支持信用分配、故障定位等分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言环境（如浏览器、终端、交互模拟器）仅输出原始文本，不提供MDP分析所需的显式状态空间、观测到状态的映射、认证转移和终止条件，导致现有强化学习与规划方法难以直接应用。

Method: 提出SDP框架：智能体在每一步提交一个描述期望世界状态的自然语言谓词，执行动作使该谓词为真，然后检查观测是否匹配该谓词。通过谓词通过/失败来构建认证状态、状态空间、观测映射、转移函数和终止条件。

Result: 在规划、科学探索、Web推理和多跳问答五个基准上，SDP均取得最佳无训练结果，且优势随任务长度增加而扩大。认证轨迹还支持谓词级信用分配、失败定位、部分进度测量和模块化算子替换等分析。

Conclusion: SDP为语言环境提供了缺失的MDP结构，使无训练推理任务性能显著提升，并赋予传统反应式智能体无法实现的可解释性分析能力。

Abstract: Language environments such as web browsers, code terminals, and interactive simulations emit raw text rather than states, and provide none of the runtime structure that MDP analysis requires. No explicit state space, no observation-to-state mapping, no certified transitions, and no termination criterion. We introduce the State-Centric Decision Process (SDP), a runtime framework that constructs these missing inputs by having the agent build them, predicate by predicate, as it acts. At each step the agent commits to a natural-language predicate describing how the world should look, takes an action to make it true, and checks the observation against it. Predicates that pass become certified states, and the resulting trajectory carries the four objects language environments do not provide, namely a task-induced state space, an observation-to-state mapping, certified transitions, and a termination criterion. We evaluate SDP on five benchmarks spanning planning, scientific exploration, web reasoning, and multi-hop question answering. SDP achieves the best training-free results on all five, with the advantage widening as the horizon grows. The certified trajectories additionally support analyses unavailable to reactive agents, including per-predicate credit assignment, failure localization, partial-progress measurement, and modular operator replacement.

</details>


### [219] [PROMETHEUS: Automating Deep Causal Research Integrating Text, Data and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12835)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PROMETHEUS是一个将文献、数据、代码等科学资源转化为因果图谱（causal atlases）的框架，通过局部因果预测模型、限制映射和粘合诊断构建可导航的Topos World Model，支持在多个案例中评估因果主张的一致性与矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能够从文本中提取局部因果主张，但这些主张以扁平摘要形式存在时缺乏实用性，需要被组织为持久、可导航的世界模型，才能支持深入的因果研究与分析。

Method: PROMETHEUS将检索到的文献、数据、代码等资源转化为因果图谱（causal atlases），即研究基底（research substrate）上显式覆盖（explicit cover）的局部因果预测状态模型族。每个局部区域包含因果事件、结构化主张表、预测测试、支持统计和来源信息；通过限制映射（restriction maps）比较重叠区域，利用粘合诊断（gluing diagnostics）揭示一致性、漂移、矛盾和不充分决定。

Result: 通过三个文献图谱案例（海洋温度对海洋种群的影响、GLP-1减肥证据、白藜芦醇/红酒健康益处主张）展示了从文本中进行深度因果研究的能力，包括明确的位置性、证据、持久状态和粘合张力。通过四个基于反事实的案例（Nature Climate Change微塑料强迫论文、Indus Valley水文论文、Sachs蛋白信号研究、Nature唱歌小鼠研究）展示了当论文附带源数据、模拟输出或代码时，PROMETHEUS能够评估反事实并围绕结果重建层世界模型。

Conclusion: PROMETHEUS提供了一种研究工具，用于导航语料库说了什么、在哪里说的、支持强度如何，以及局部主张在何处未能整合为一致的全局视图。Topos World Model并非单一的通用图，而是用于探索因果知识结构及其边界的研究仪器。

Abstract: Large language models can extract local causal claims from text, but those claims become more useful when organized as persistent, navigable world models rather than as flat summaries. We introduce PROMETHEUS, a framework that turns retrieved literature, filings, reviews, reports, agent traces, source data, code, simulations, and scientific models into causal atlases: sheaf-like families of local causal predictive-state models over an explicit cover of a research substrate. Each local region contains causal episodes, structured claim tables, predictive tests, support statistics, and provenance; restriction maps compare overlapping regions; gluing diagnostics expose agreement, drift, contradiction, and underdetermination. The resulting Topos World Model is not a single universal graph. It is a research instrument for navigating what a corpus says, where it says it, how strongly it is supported, and where local claims fail to assemble into a coherent global view. Three literature-atlas case studies -- ocean-temperature impacts on marine populations, GLP-1 weight-loss evidence, and resveratrol/red-wine health-benefit claims -- illustrate deep causal research from text with explicit locality, evidence, persistent state, and gluing tension. Four grounded-counterfactual case studies -- a Nature Climate Change microplastics forcing paper, an Indus Valley hydrology paper with VIC-derived figure data and model code, the canonical Sachs protein-signaling study with single-cell perturbation data, and a Nature singing-mouse study with MAPseq projection matrices -- show a stronger mode: when a paper ships source data, simulation outputs, or code, PROMETHEUS can evaluate a counterfactual against that scientific substrate and then rebuild the sheaf world model around the

</details>


### [220] [Multimodal Hidden Markov Models for Persistent Emotional State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12838)
*Anamika Ragu,Aneesh Jonelagadda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个轻量级框架，利用粘性阶乘HDP-HMM对多模态（视频、音频、文本）的效价-唤醒度表征进行建模，将对话情感分解为可解释的潜在情感阶段序列，并以更低的计算成本优于基线高斯HMM，同时通过上下文增强改善LLM在情感不稳定阶段的响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感识别方法仅在话语级别操作，无法捕捉对话中持续的情感阶段动态，这在临床等应用场景中尤为重要。需要一个既能建模对话情感动态可解释变化、又足够轻量且可规模化的框架。

Method: 采用粘性阶乘HDP-HMM（Sticky Factorial HDP-HMM）对从视频、音频、文本多模态信号提取的效价-唤醒度（valence-arousal）表征进行时序建模，将对话情感分解为一系列潜在情感阶段（emotional regimes）。评估使用LLM作为评判者（LLM-as-a-Judge）、几何一致性和时间一致性指标。

Result: 粘性HDP-HMM比基线高斯HMM产生更可解释的情感阶段序列，且计算成本远低于基于LLM的对话状态追踪方法。在临床数据集的问答实验中，从多模态效价-唤醒度轨迹中恢复的情感阶段可通过上下文增强改善LLM在情感不稳定阶段的回答质量。

Conclusion: 该框架为可解释、轻量级、可规模化的对话情感动态分析开辟了一条新路径，具有临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Tracking an interpretable emotional arc of a conversation via the sentiment of individual utterances processed as a whole is central to both understanding and guiding communication in applied, especially clinical, conversational contexts. Existing approaches to emotion recognition operate at the utterance level, obscuring the persistent phases that characterize real conversational dynamics. We propose a lightweight framework that models conversational emotion as a sequence of latent emotional regimes using sticky factorial HDP-HMMs over multimodal valence-arousal representations derived from simultaneous video, audio and textual input. We evaluate the quality of regime prediction using LLM-as-a-Judge, geometric, and temporal consistency metrics, demonstrating that the sticky HDP-HMM produces more interpretable regime sequences than the baseline Gaussian HMM at a fraction of the computational cost of LLM-based dialogue state tracking methods. In addition, Question-Answer experiments in a clinical dataset suggest that meaningful emotional phases can reliably be recovered from multimodal valence-arousal trajectories and used to improve the quality of LLM responses in unstable affective regimes via context augmentation. This framework thus opens a path toward interpretable, lightweight, and actionable analysis of conversational emotion dynamics at scale.

</details>


### [221] [Moltbook Moderation: Uncovering Hidden Intent Through Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12856)
*Ali Al-Lawati,Nafis Tripto,Abolfazl Ansari,Jason Lucas,Suhang Wang,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bot-Mod，一个基于智能体意图而非内容本身的审核框架，通过Gibbs采样驱动的多轮对话交互来识别恶意意图，实验表明其在多种对抗配置下能可靠识别恶意行为并保持低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中的恶意智能体可能发布表面上无害的内容以规避基于内容的审核，同时通过整体交互模式实施剥削性和恶意行为，传统内容过滤方法无法应对此类威胁。

Method: Bot-Mod通过与被审核智能体进行多轮对话交互，利用基于Gibbs采样的候选意图假设空间搜索，逐步缩小可能的智能体目标范围以识别其潜在行为意图。

Result: 基于Moltbook构建的数据集实验表明，Bot-Mod在多种对抗配置下能够可靠识别智能体意图，同时对良性行为保持较低的误报率。

Conclusion: 该工作为开放多智能体环境中可扩展的、基于意图的审核奠定了基础。

Abstract: The emergence of multi-agent systems introduces novel moderation challenges that extend beyond content filtering. Agents with {\em malicious intent} may contribute harmful content that appears benign to evade content-based moderation, while compromising the system through exploitative and malicious behavior manifested across their overall interaction patterns within the community. To address this, we introduce \textsc{\textbf{Bot-Mod}} (\textsc{\textbf{Bot-Mod}}eration), a moderation framework that grounds detection in agent intent rather than traditional content level signals. \method{} identifies the underlying intent by engaging with the target agent in a multi-turn exchange guided by Gibbs-based sampling over candidate intent hypotheses. This progressively narrows the space of plausible agent objectives to identify the underlying behavior. To evaluate our approach, we construct a dataset derived from Moltbook that encompasses diverse benign and malicious behaviors based on actual community structures, posts, and comments. Results demonstrate that \textsc{\textbf{Bot-Mod}} reliably identifies agent intent across a range of adversarial configurations, while maintaining a low false positive rate on benign behaviors. This work advances the foundation for scalable, intent-aware moderation of agents in open multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond Cooperative Simulators: Generating Realistic User Personas for Robust Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12894)
